	local environment = state.context.environment
	local colors = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 90), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 90, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 255, 120)}
	local parts = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)

	local original = {}
	for index, part in parts do original[index] = {frame = part.CFrame, size = part.Size} end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whistle, 0.6, 0.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.8, function(progress)
		local squared = progress * progress
		local scale = 1 + 0.55 * squared + math.sin(progress * 32) * 0.05 * progress
		local rise = environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.4 * squared, 0)
		for index, part in parts do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local base = original[index]
			part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest + (base.frame.Position - effect.chest) * (1 + 0.4 * squared) + rise) * base.frame.Rotation
			part.Size = base.size * scale
		end
	end, function()
		local position = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.4, 0)
		for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end

		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(2, 3, 2))
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent = anchor
		local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
		local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		for _, color in colors do
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color),
				Size = sequence(0, 0.24, 1, 0.18), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.3, 2.1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 22),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2.5, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -14, 0),
				Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-400, 400),
				Squash = sequence(0, 0, 0.2, 1.5, 0.4, -1.5, 0.6, 1.5, 0.8, -1.5, 1, 0),
				Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.2, LightInfluence = 0.6, Brightness = 1.6}, 14)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
			Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 9), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Brightness = 4}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 7), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.25), Brightness = 3}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.balloonPop, 1, 1.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.partyHorn, 0.8, 1.3, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whistle, 0.5, 1.4, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 0.5, 0.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.6)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.scatterBody(state, body, center, minimum, variation, lift, spin)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	GameEffects.Kill.removeSockets(body)
	for _, part in body.body do
		local direction = part.Position - center
		direction = direction.Magnitude > 0.05 and direction.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
		part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * (minimum + math.random() * variation) + environment.Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)
		part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, spin)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.removeSockets(body)
	for _, joint in body.model:GetDescendants() do
		if joint:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then joint:Destroy() end
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.shred(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)

	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 4.4, 1.4))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local angles = {30, 150, 80, -20}
	for index = 1, 4 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.075, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, (index - 2.5) * 0.35, 0), angles[index] + math.random(-12, 12),
				3 + math.random() * 0.6, 1.1, color, 0.6, 0.05, 0.07, math.random(-25, 25), nil, index % 2 == 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 0),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.15, 0.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(16, 28), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
				Drag = 6, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 10)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, index % 2 == 0 and GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshAiry or GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.stab1, 0.8, 0.95 + index * 0.12, anchor)
		end)
	end

	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.45, function()
		if body then body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), function(result) GameEffects.Kill.scatterBody(state, result, effect.chest, 7, 6, 9, 8) end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
			Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)), Size = sequence(0, 0.7, 1, 0.25),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 20), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -65, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200),
			Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.1}, 60)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.burst,
			Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 70), environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 12)), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 8),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Brightness = 3}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rip, 0.9, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts2, 0.9, 0.9, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.7, 0.25)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.25, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6.5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 3.2, effect.ignore, 1.1)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 14, 0.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, strength)
	if not body or not body.torso then return nil end

	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local mass = 0
	for _, part in body.body do mass += part:GetMass() end
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = body.torso
	local force = environment.Instance.new("VectorForce")
	force.Attachment0, force.RelativeTo, force.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = attachment, environment.Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World, true
	force.Force, force.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0, mass * context.workspace.Gravity * strength, 0), body.torso
	return force
end

function GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position, lifetime)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, lifetime or 4)
	return anchor, attachment
end

function GameEffects.Kill.bubble(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 1), 4.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if not body or not body.torso then return end

	local force = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 1.02)
	local shell = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	shell.Shape = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball
	shell.Anchored, shell.CanCollide, shell.CanQuery, shell.CanTouch, shell.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
	shell.Material, shell.Color = environment.Enum.Material.Glass, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 240, 255)
	shell.Transparency, shell.Reflectance = 0.7, 0.4
	shell.Size, shell.CFrame, shell.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), environment.CFrame.new(body.torso.Position), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local shine = shell:Clone()
	shine.Material, shine.Color = environment.Enum.Material.ForceField, environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 130, 255)
	shine.Transparency, shine.Reflectance, shine.Parent = 0, 0, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)

	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 2, function(progress)
		if not body.torso.Parent then return end
		local time = progress * 2
		local diameter = math.min(1, time / 0.3) * 6.5 + math.sin(time * 6) * 0.2
		shell.Size = environment.Vector3.new(diameter, diameter * (1 + math.sin(time * 5) * 0.03), diameter)
		shine.Size = shell.Size * 1.01
		shell.CFrame, shine.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(body.torso.Position), environment.CFrame.new(body.torso.Position)
	end, function()
		local position = shell.Position
		for _, part in {shell, shine} do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		if force then pcall(function() force:Destroy() end) end

		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
		local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		local blue = environment.Color3.fromRGB(210, 240, 255)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(blue, blue),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.35, 1, 0.1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 16), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 2}, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 3, 1, 9), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.2, 1, 1),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Brightness = 3}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.balloonPop, 1, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.waterSplash, 0.7, 1.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bubblesDeep, 0.6, 1, nil, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fairy, 0.4, 1.4, nil, 1.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.paintRays(state, effect, direction, first, second)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state), context.player.Character}

	for index = 1, 10 do
		local offset = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() * 0.9 - 0.6, math.random() - 0.5) * 1.4
		local result = context.workspace:Raycast(effect.chest, (direction + offset).Unit * 16, params)
		if not result or result.Instance.Transparency >= 0.9 then continue end

		local model = result.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then continue end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, result.Distance / 45, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.groundDecal(state, result.Position, result.Normal, 1.2 + math.random() * 1.8,
				index % 2 == 0 and first or second, 3.2, 0.1, state.textures.splat, 2)
		end)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.paint(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local palette = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 255),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 40), environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 90),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40)}
	local index = math.random(1, #palette)
	local other = (index % #palette + math.random(1, #palette - 1) - 1) % #palette + 1
	local first, second = palette[index], palette[other]
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, direction * 18 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 3), 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)

	if body then
		local highlight = environment.Instance.new("Highlight")
		highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor, highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = first, second, 0.3, 0.4
		highlight.DepthMode, highlight.Adornee, highlight.Parent = environment.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded, body.model, body.model
	end

	local up = math.abs(direction.Y) < 0.95 and environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(effect.chest, direction:Cross(up).Unit, direction), environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(a, b) return environment.ColorSequence.new(a, b or a) end
	for colorIndex, color in {first, second} do
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(color), Size = sequence(0, 0.7, 1, 0.3),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(12, 26), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(65, 65),
			Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -50, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200),
			LightEmission = 0.1, LightInfluence = 0.4, Brightness = 1.5}, colorIndex == 1 and 30 or 20)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.burst, Color = colors(first, second), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 6),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.25), Brightness = 2.5}, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.paintRays(state, effect, direction, first, second)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function()
		local position = body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 3.2, first, 3, effect.ignore, 0.3, state.textures.splat, 2)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.mud, 1, 1.1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bounce, 0.6, 1.4, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.punch, 0.5, 1.4, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.4, 0.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.reaper(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 255, 160)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 4, 1.6))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0), -15, 4.4, 1.35, color, 1.2, 0.13, 0.12, 25,
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(215, 255, 230), math.random() < 0.5)
	local shadow = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
		color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(12, 10, 16), transparency = 0})
	local soul = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.ForceField, color = color, transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)

	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function()
		for _, part in shadow do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 1, {CFrame = part.CFrame - environment.Vector3.new(0, 5.5, 0), Transparency = 0.6},
				environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 1.05)
		end
	end)
	local frames = {}
	for index, part in soul do frames[index] = part.CFrame end
	local angle = math.random() * 6.28
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.15, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.5, function(progress)
			local eased = 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 2
			local rotation = angle + eased * 7
			local offset = environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(rotation) * 1.4 * eased, eased * 7, math.sin(rotation) * 1.4 * eased)
			for index, part in soul do part.CFrame, part.Transparency = frames[index] + offset, progress ^ 1.6 end
		end, function() for _, part in soul do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end)
	end)

	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 80, 50)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 7, 0), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(40, 40), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		Brightness = 3}, 30, 0.1)
	local floor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.15, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(4, 0.2, 4))
	local floorAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	floorAttachment.Parent = floor
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, floor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB,
		FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4, FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot,
		Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(22, 18, 28)), Size = sequence(0, 2.5, 1, 5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 0.7, 0.4, 1, 1),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.9, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60),
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 12)
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, floorAttachment, state.textures.vortex, color, 2, 9, 1.1, 3)
	ring.RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-220)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 4, 14, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicGrowl, 1, 0.55, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whisper, 0.6, 0.8, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, floor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.blade(state, color, material, length, width, transparency)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local parts = {}
	local specs = {
		{"WedgePart", environment.Vector3.new(0.06, length, width / 2), color, material},
		{"WedgePart", environment.Vector3.new(0.06, length, width / 2), color, material},
		{"Part", environment.Vector3.new(0.14, 0.1, width + 0.3), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 36, 34), environment.Enum.Material.Metal},
		{"Part", environment.Vector3.new(0.14, 0.7, 0.17), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 34, 20), environment.Enum.Material.Wood},
	}
	for _, spec in specs do
		local part = environment.Instance.new(spec[1])
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
		part.Size, part.Color, part.Material, part.Transparency = spec[2], spec[3], spec[4], transparency or 0
		part.Reflectance, part.Parent = spec[4] == environment.Enum.Material.Metal and 0.35 or 0, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(parts, part)
	end
	return parts, {environment.CFrame.new(0, length / 2, -width / 4),
		environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) * environment.CFrame.new(0, length / 2, -width / 4),
		environment.CFrame.new(0, -0.05, 0), environment.CFrame.new(0, -0.45, 0)}
end

function GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position, direction)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local reference = math.abs(direction.Z) < 0.9 and environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	return environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(position, direction:Cross(reference).Unit, direction)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(parts, offsets, frame)
	for index, part in parts do if part.Parent then part.CFrame = frame * offsets[index] end end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.butterflies(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local palette = {
		{environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 210), environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 80, 255)},
		{environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 215, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 110, 255)},
		{environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 60)},
		{environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 205), environment.Color3.fromRGB(50, 195, 170)},
		{environment.Color3.fromRGB(215, 175, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 190)},
	}
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local highlight = model and GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, palette[1][1], white, 1, 0.35)
	if highlight then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.35, {FillTransparency = 0.45}) end
	local sorted = table.clone(body)
	table.sort(sorted, function(first, second) return first.Position.Y < second.Position.Y end)
	local rotation = environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
	local butterflies, created = {}, {}
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local function wing(size, color, transparency)
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Shape, part.Anchored = environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, true
		part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = false, false, false, false
		part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, transparency
		part.Size, part.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.02, size, size), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(created, part)
		return part
	end
	local function spawn(position)
		if #butterflies >= 20 then return end
		local colors, scale = palette[math.random(1, #palette)], 0.8 + math.random() * 0.5
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = false, false, false, false
		part.Material, part.Color = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 30, 50)
		part.Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.07 * scale, 0.07 * scale, 0.38 * scale)
		part.CFrame, part.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(position), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(created, part)
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, part, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, colors[1]),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.1),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Brightness = 4}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 9, true
		local butterfly = {position = position, phase = math.random() * 6.28, wobble = math.random() * 6.28,
			velocity = environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 3, 2 + math.random() * 2, (math.random() - 0.5) * 3), body = part}
		butterfly.wings = {
			{part = wing(0.62 * scale, colors[1], 0.1), side = -1, z = -0.1 * scale, radius = 0.3 * scale},
			{part = wing(0.62 * scale, colors[1], 0.1), side = 1, z = -0.1 * scale, radius = 0.3 * scale},
			{part = wing(0.44 * scale, colors[2], 0.12), side = -1, z = 0.15 * scale, radius = 0.22 * scale},
			{part = wing(0.44 * scale, colors[2], 0.12), side = 1, z = 0.15 * scale, radius = 0.22 * scale},
		}
		table.insert(butterflies, butterfly)
	end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3))
	local previous, nextPart = 0, 1
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 3.2, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 3.2
		local delta = elapsed - previous
		previous = elapsed
		while nextPart <= #sorted and elapsed >= 0.3 + nextPart / #sorted * 0.9 do
			local part = sorted[nextPart]
			nextPart += 1
			if part.Parent then
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 1})
				for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, child, 0.25, {Transparency = 1}) end end
				anchor.CFrame, anchor.Size = part.CFrame, part.Size
				spawn(part.Position)
			end
		end
		local parts, frames = {}, {}
		for _, butterfly in butterflies do
			local velocity = butterfly.velocity
			butterfly.velocity = (velocity + environment.Vector3.new(math.sin(elapsed * 2.2 + butterfly.wobble) * 4,
				1.3 + math.sin(elapsed * 3 + butterfly.phase) * 1.6, math.cos(elapsed * 1.9 + butterfly.wobble) * 4) * delta) * (1 - 0.35 * delta)
			butterfly.position += butterfly.velocity * delta
			local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(butterfly.velocity.X, 0, butterfly.velocity.Z)
			local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(butterfly.position, butterfly.position + (horizontal.Magnitude > 0.1 and horizontal or environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)))
				* environment.CFrame.Angles(-math.pi / 10, 0, 0)
			local flap = (math.sin((elapsed + butterfly.phase) * 24) * 0.5 + 0.5) * 1.35
			table.insert(parts, butterfly.body)
			table.insert(frames, frame)
			local fade = math.clamp((elapsed - 2.5) / 0.7, 0, 1)
			for _, item in butterfly.wings do
				table.insert(parts, item.part)
				table.insert(frames, frame * environment.CFrame.new(item.side * 0.03, 0, item.z) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, item.side * flap)
					* environment.CFrame.new(item.side * item.radius, 0, 0) * rotation)
				if fade > 0 then item.part.Transparency = 0.1 + 0.9 * fade end
			end
			if fade > 0 then butterfly.body.Transparency = fade end
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
	end, function()
		for _, part in created do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
	end)
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, palette[5][1]),
		Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 30, true
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.3, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, palette[1][1], 3, 12, 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fairy, 0.8, 1, nil, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.shimmer, 0.5, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.pigeonWings, 0.35, 1.5, nil, 1.6) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.4, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.4, 1.2, nil, 2) end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.knifeRain(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, -2, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 1), 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local targets = {}
	if body then
		for _, name in {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperArm"} do
			local part = body.model:FindFirstChild(name)
			if part then table.insert(targets, part) end
		end
	end
	local hits = 0
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	for index = 1, 11 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.07, function()
			if not state.alive then return end
			local target = index % 2 == 1 and targets[(index + 1) / 2]
			local position
			if target then
				position = target.Position + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5, 0.3, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3)
			else
				local angle, radius = math.random() * 6.28, 1.4 + math.random() * 2.4
				local offset = environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
				local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root + offset, effect.ignore)
				position = ground and ground - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0) or effect.root + offset - environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
			local direction = environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5, -1, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5).Unit
			local parts, offsets = GameEffects.Kill.blade(state, environment.Color3.fromRGB(210, 214, 224), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, 1.7, 0.36)
			local finish = GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position - direction * (1.7 - 0.45), direction)
			local start = finish - direction * 16
			GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(parts, offsets, start)
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.2, function(progress)
				GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(parts, offsets, start:Lerp(finish, progress * progress))
			end, function()
				local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 160)),
					Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.15, 0.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 16),
					SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 5, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 4}, 8)
				if target and target.Parent then
					for _, part in parts do
						part.Anchored, part.Massless, part.Parent = false, true, body.model
						local weld = environment.Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
						weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.Parent = target, part, part
					end
					GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 20), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
						Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0.15), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12),
						SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -55, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 14)
					GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.7, function() for _, part in parts do if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end end end)
					hits += 1
					if hits == 2 then body.release() end
					GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.stab1, 0.7, 1.3 + math.random() * 0.3, anchor)
				else
					local gray = environment.Color3.fromRGB(140, 135, 130)
					GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(gray), Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 1, 2),
						Transparency = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5),
						SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(70, 70), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 5)
					GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.arrow2, 0.6, 0.6 + math.random() * 0.3, anchor)
					GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.2, function() for _, part in parts do if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end end end)
					for _, part in parts do GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 2.7) end
				end
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1)
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.arrow, 0.35, 1.6 + math.random() * 0.4)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function() if body and not body.released then body.release() end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function()
		local position = body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 4, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.6, effect.ignore, 1)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.judgment(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 140), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if model then GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 220), 0.55, 0) end
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local beam = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	beam.Shape, beam.Anchored, beam.CanCollide, beam.CanQuery, beam.CanTouch, beam.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, true, false, false, false, false
	beam.Material, beam.Color, beam.Transparency = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, 0.55
	beam.Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.2, 0.2)
	beam.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 80, effect.root.Z) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
	beam.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 5.5, 5.5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2, function()
		if beam.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.5, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.1, 0.1), Transparency = 1}) end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 2.6)
	local adornments = {}
	for index = 0, 15 do
		local first, second = index / 16 * 6.283, (index + 1) / 16 * 6.283
		local start = effect.head + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(first) * 0.8, 1.1, math.sin(first) * 0.8)
		local finish = effect.head + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(second) * 0.8, 1.1, math.sin(second) * 0.8)
		table.insert(adornments, GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.12, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 120), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0))
	end
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local wingCenter = effect.chest + direction * 0.6 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)
	local wings = {}
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		for index = 1, 4 do
			local part = environment.Instance.new("WedgePart")
			part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
			part.Size, part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = environment.Vector3.new(0.08, 2.4 + index * 0.5, 0.7), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 240), 0.15
			part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			table.insert(wings, {part = part, side = side, index = index})
			table.insert(adornments, part)
		end
	end
	local bodyFrames, haloFrames = {}, {}
	for index, part in body do bodyFrames[index] = part.CFrame end
	local function wingFrame(wing, spread)
		local angle = math.rad(20 + wing.index * 22) * spread
		return environment.CFrame.lookAt(wingCenter, wingCenter - direction) * environment.CFrame.new(wing.side * 0.3, 0, 0)
			* environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -wing.side * (angle + 0.15)) * environment.CFrame.new(0, (2.4 + wing.index * 0.5) / 2, 0)
			* environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0)
	end
	for _, wing in wings do wing.part.CFrame = wingFrame(wing, 0) end
	for index, part in adornments do haloFrames[index] = part.CFrame end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 2, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 2
		local spread = math.clamp(elapsed / 0.4, 0, 1)
		local rise = elapsed < 0.5 and 0 or ((elapsed - 0.5) / 1.5) ^ 2 * 14
		local offset, parts, frames = environment.Vector3.new(0, rise, 0), {}, {}
		for index, part in body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			table.insert(parts, part)
			table.insert(frames, bodyFrames[index] + offset)
		end
		for _, wing in wings do
			local flap = math.sin(elapsed * 9) * 0.12 * spread
			table.insert(parts, wing.part)
			table.insert(frames, wingFrame(wing, spread + flap) + offset)
		end
		for index = 1, 16 do table.insert(parts, adornments[index]); table.insert(frames, haloFrames[index] + offset) end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
	end, function()
		local position = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 14, 0)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, position, color, 14, 6)
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(2, 3, 2))
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.leaf, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.45, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.5, 2.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 8),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 1.5, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -3, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.4, LightInfluence = 0.5, Brightness = 2}, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
		for _, group in {body, adornments} do for _, part in group do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.7, 1.2, nil, 2)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(3, 5, 3))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.needles, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, white),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 5, 0.5, 8, 1, 9), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.3, 1, 1),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.6), Brightness = 3, ZOffset = -1}, 1)
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.leaf, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.35, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.5, 2.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.5),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -2, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-150, 150),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.3, LightInfluence = 0.5, Brightness = 2}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 16, true
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.8, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 20, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 4)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.choir, 0.6, 1, nil, 2.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fairy, 0.5, 1.5, nil, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.vampire(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local pale = GameEffects.Kill.transmute(state, body, environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 165, 162), 0.9, false, true)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2, 4, 1.4))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 25)), Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 4),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.4, Brightness = 1.5}, 8)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function()
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for _, part in pale do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.5, {Size = part.Size * 0.3, CFrame = part.CFrame - environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), Transparency = 1},
				environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, child, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end end
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.55)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 185, 180), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 88, 86)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 0.05), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2.5),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -7, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
			ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 60)
	end)
	local function target()
		if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then return effect.killer.Position end
		return effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)
	end
	for index = 1, 16 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08 + index * 0.035, function()
			if not state.alive then return end
			local orb = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			orb.Shape, orb.Anchored, orb.CanCollide, orb.CanQuery, orb.CanTouch, orb.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
			orb.Material, orb.Color, orb.Size = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 30), environment.Vector3.new(0.32, 0.32, 0.32)
			local first, second = environment.Instance.new("Attachment"), environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
			first.Position, first.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.14, 0), orb
			second.Position, second.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0, -0.14, 0), orb
			local trail = environment.Instance.new("Trail")
			trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1, trail.Lifetime = first, second, 0.22 / state.speed
			trail.Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 80), environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 20))
			trail.Transparency, trail.WidthScale = sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1), sequence(0, 1, 1, 0.2)
			trail.LightEmission, trail.FaceCamera, trail.Parent = 1, true, orb
			local start = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2.4, (math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6)
			orb.CFrame, orb.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(start), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			local drift = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0, math.random() - 0.5) * 6
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.55 + math.random() * 0.3, function(progress)
				local finish = target()
				local control = (start + finish) / 2 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3 + (finish - start).Magnitude * 0.15, 0) + drift
				orb.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(GameEffects.Kill.bezier(start, control, finish, progress ^ 1.6))
			end, function() orb.Transparency = 1; GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, orb, 0.3) end)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.05, function()
		local burst = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(target()), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent = burst
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 70)), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 7),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4), Brightness = 4}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60)), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 5),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5), Brightness = 2}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 80)), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 8), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Brightness = 4}, 12)
		GameEffects.Kill.light(state, burst, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60), 4, 10, 0.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicGlow, 0.35, 1.6, burst)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, burst, 1.5)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 30), 3, 12, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.stab2, 0.9, 0.8, anchor)
	for _, delay in {0.35, 0.7} do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, delay, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.punch2, 0.5, 0.45, anchor) end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.iaido(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.8, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local forward, right = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local upper = body and body.model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	local lower = body and body.model:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
	local head = body and body.head
	local socket = GameEffects.Kill.findSocket(body, upper, lower)
	local center = socket and socket.Attachment0 and socket.Attachment0.WorldPosition or effect.chest - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.7, 0)
	for index, position in {center, effect.head - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)} do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.05, function()
			local direction = (right + environment.Vector3.new(0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25, 0)).Unit
			local start, finish = position - direction * 24, position + direction * 24
			local beams = {GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.2, white),
				GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.7, environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 215, 255), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.55)}
			for _, beam in beams do
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.35, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, beam.Size.Z)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.4)
			end
		end)
	end
	local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.speed, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 6, 1, 9),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 3, ZOffset = 2}, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 5, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.15), Brightness = 6, ZOffset = 2}, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.swordIn, 1, 1.9)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.58, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.timerBell, 1, 0.7) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.66, function()
		if not body then return end
		body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), function(result)
			GameEffects.Kill.cutSocket(result, upper, lower)
			GameEffects.Kill.cutSocket(result, head, upper)
			for _, part in result.body do
				if part == head then
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(0, 14, 0) + right * 4
					part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8)
					continue
				end
				if environment.UPPER[part.Name] then
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = right * 5 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
					continue
				end
				local side = part.Name:find("Left") and -1 or 1
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = -right * 5 + forward * side * 3 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
			if lower then lower.AssemblyAngularVelocity = forward * (math.random() < 0.5 and -9 or 9) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, upper, false, body.model)
		GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, lower, true, body.model)
		GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, upper, true, body.model)
		GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, head, false, body.model)
		local anchor, burst = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 22),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.1}, 50)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bone2, 1, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.spurt, 0.9, 1, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.7, 0.25)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function()
			local position = body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.8, effect.ignore, 1.1)
		end)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.sever(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local upper = body and body.model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	local lower = body and body.model:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
	local socket = GameEffects.Kill.findSocket(body, upper, lower)
	local center = socket and socket.Attachment0 and socket.Attachment0.WorldPosition or effect.chest - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.7, 0)
	local forward, right = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local side = math.random() < 0.5 and -1 or 1
	local normal, radius, halfAngle = -forward, 4.6, 0.95
	GameEffects.Kill.arcFrom(state, center - normal * radius * math.cos(halfAngle), normal, right * side, radius, -halfAngle, halfAngle, color, 0.95, 0.08, 0.12)
	local line = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, center - right * 5, center + right * 5, 0.07, white)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, line, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 12), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, line, 0.3)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.lookAt(center, center + right), environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 3))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.9, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.15, 0.35), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 24), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
		Drag = 6, Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 24, 0.04)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 20), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.2), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 14),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(80, 80), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 30, 0.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.1, function()
		if not body then return end
		body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), function(result)
			GameEffects.Kill.cutSocket(result, upper, lower)
			for _, part in result.body do
				if environment.UPPER[part.Name] then
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = right * side * 9 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
					continue
				end
				local sign = part.Name:find("Left") and -1 or 1
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = -right * side * 5 + forward * sign * 3 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
			if upper then upper.AssemblyAngularVelocity = forward * side * 3 end
			if lower then lower.AssemblyAngularVelocity = right * (math.random() < 0.5 and -9 or 9) end
		end)
		if upper and lower then
			GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, upper, false, body.model)
			GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, lower, true, body.model)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function()
			local position = body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 4.5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.8, effect.ignore, 1.1)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bone1, 0.9, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts, 0.8, 0.9, anchor)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 14, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.swordWhip, 1, 1.1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.swordIn, 0.8, 1.2, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.5, 0.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.findSocket(body, first, second)
	if not body or not first or not second then return nil end
	for _, joint in body.model:GetDescendants() do
		if not joint:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then continue end
		local left = joint.Attachment0 and joint.Attachment0.Parent
		local right = joint.Attachment1 and joint.Attachment1.Parent
		if left == first and right == second or left == second and right == first then return joint end
	end
	return nil
end

function GameEffects.Kill.cutSocket(body, first, second)
	if not body or not first or not second then return end
	for _, joint in body.model:GetDescendants() do
		if not joint:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then continue end
		local left = joint.Attachment0 and joint.Attachment0.Parent
		local right = joint.Attachment1 and joint.Attachment1.Parent
		if left == first and right == second or left == second and right == first then joint:Destroy() end
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.wound(state, part, top, parent)
	if not part or not part.Parent then return nil end
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local wound = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	wound.Size = environment.Vector3.new(part.Size.X * 0.8, 0.05, part.Size.Z * 0.8)
	wound.Material, wound.Color = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(160, 0, 14)
	wound.CanCollide, wound.CanQuery, wound.CanTouch, wound.Massless, wound.CastShadow = false, false, false, true, false
	wound.CFrame = part.CFrame * environment.CFrame.new(0, (top and 1 or -1) * (part.Size.Y / 2 - 0.02), 0)
	wound.Parent = parent or part.Parent
	local weld = environment.Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
	weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.Parent = part, wound, wound
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, wound, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
		Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 0, 25), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 10)),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.35, 1, 0.14), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 11), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(25, 25), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -55, 0),
		EmissionDirection = top and environment.Enum.NormalId.Top or environment.Enum.NormalId.Bottom, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 8)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 55, true
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.1, function()
		if wound.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, wound, 0.5, {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 0, 5), Transparency = 1}) end
	end)
	return wound
end

function GameEffects.Kill.dragon(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, center = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30), effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local segments, horns = {}, {}
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local flameColors = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 90), environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 10))
	for index = 1, 18 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Shape, part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
		local size = index == 1 and 1.7 or 1.35 - index / 18 * 0.95
		part.Size, part.Material = environment.Vector3.new(size, size, size), environment.Enum.Material.Neon
		part.Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 90):Lerp(environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 30, 10), index / 18)
		part.CFrame, part.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(center + environment.Vector3.new(0, -500, 0)), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		if index % 2 == 1 then
			local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, part, {Texture = state.textures.flame, Color = flameColors, Size = sequence(0, size * 0.9, 1, 0.1),
				Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.25, 0.45), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 3}, 0)
			emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 25, true
		end
		table.insert(segments, part)
	end
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("WedgePart")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
		part.Size, part.Material, part.Color = environment.Vector3.new(0.25, 1.1, 0.5), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 180)
		part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(horns, {part = part, side = side})
	end
	local angle = math.random() * 6.28
	local function position(progress)
		if progress < 0.8 then
			local stage = progress / 0.8
			local phase, radius = angle + stage * 9.5, 8 * (1 - stage) + 0.2
			return center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(phase) * radius, 6 - stage * 6.2, math.sin(phase) * radius)
		end
		local stage = (progress - 0.8) / 0.2
		return center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * stage * 2, -0.2 + stage * 26, math.sin(angle) * stage * 2)
	end
	local struck = false
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.6, function(progress)
		local parts, frames = {}, {}
		for index, part in segments do
			local stage = math.clamp(progress - (index - 1) * 0.022, 0, 1)
			local current, ahead = position(stage), position(math.min(1, stage + 0.01))
			local frame = (ahead - current).Magnitude > 0.001 and environment.CFrame.lookAt(current, ahead) or environment.CFrame.new(current)
			table.insert(parts, part)
			table.insert(frames, frame)
			if index == 1 then
				for _, horn in horns do
					table.insert(parts, horn.part)
					table.insert(frames, frame * environment.CFrame.new(horn.side * 0.45, 0.6, 0.3) * environment.CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-50), 0, horn.side * 0.3))
				end
			end
			if progress > 0.85 then part.Transparency = (progress - 0.85) / 0.15 end
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
		if struck or progress < 0.8 then return end
		struck = true
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 16, 6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, center, color, 40, 40)
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(2, 3, 2))
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.flame,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 40, 10)),
			Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 0.3), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 20), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 10, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 55, 0) + GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 8, nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8) end)
			local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), 0.35, 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function()
				if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.6, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(25, 10, 5), FillTransparency = 0.1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireball2, 0.9, 1, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.6, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 1.4, 0.4)
	end, function()
		for _, part in segments do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for _, horn in horns do pcall(function() horn.part:Destroy() end) end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireWhoosh2, 0.7, 0.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.roar, 0.5, 0.45)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.impale(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 50)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local rise = environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.3, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08, function()
		for _, part in body do if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.14, {CFrame = part.CFrame + rise}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.05, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.45, function() return {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 10)} end)
	end)
	for index = 1, 9 do
		local angle, radius = index / 9 * math.pi * 2 + math.random() * 0.4, 1.8 + math.random() * 1.6
		local start = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X + math.cos(angle) * radius, height - 0.2, effect.root.Z + math.sin(angle) * radius)
		local target = effect.chest + rise + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, (math.random() - 0.3) * 1.6, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8)
		local offset = target - start
		local finish = start + offset.Unit * (offset.Magnitude + 0.6 + math.random() * 1.2)
		local dark = index % 3 == 0
		local spikes, direction, length = GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, start, finish, 0.22, 0.34 + math.random() * 0.12,
			dark and environment.Color3.fromRGB(18, 6, 10) or environment.Color3.fromRGB(105, 0, 22), dark and environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic or environment.Enum.Material.Neon)
		local delay = index * 0.02
		for _, part in spikes do
			local frame = part.CFrame
			local hidden = frame - direction * (length + 0.3)
			part.CFrame = hidden
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, delay, function() if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.13, {CFrame = frame}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end end)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.15 + delay * 0.5, function()
				if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.3, {CFrame = hidden}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 1.6 + delay)
		end
	end
	local floor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.1, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(5, 0.2, 5))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = floor
	GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.cracks, color, 3, 9, 1.2, 2)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, floor, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 60, 60)), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.3),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 18), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(35, 35),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 22)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, floor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 40, 40)),
		Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 4.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(70, 70), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 10)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest + rise), environment.Vector3.new(1.5, 2.5, 1.5))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 25), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 16),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 36, 0.14)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 14, 0.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.trap, 1, 0.8, floor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rockCrack, 0.5, 1.2, floor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.12, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.trap, 0.7, 1.1, anchor) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 1, 0.35)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, floor, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.drones(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local red, white, center = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.6, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local drones = {}
	for index = 1, 4 do
		local parts, offsets = {}, {}
		local function create(shape, size, color, material, offset)
			local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			part.Shape, part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = shape, true, false, false, false, true
			part.Size, part.Color, part.Material, part.Parent = size, color, material, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			table.insert(parts, part)
			table.insert(offsets, offset)
			return part
		end
		create(environment.Enum.PartType.Block, environment.Vector3.new(1.3, 0.35, 1.3), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 42, 48), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, environment.CFrame.new())
		local eye = create(environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, environment.Vector3.new(0.35, 0.35, 0.35), red, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.CFrame.new(0, -0.2, -0.5))
		for _, position in {environment.Vector3.new(0.8, 0.2, 0.8), environment.Vector3.new(-0.8, 0.2, 0.8),
			environment.Vector3.new(0.8, 0.2, -0.8), environment.Vector3.new(-0.8, 0.2, -0.8)} do
			create(environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.8, 0.8), environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 230, 255), environment.Enum.Material.Neon,
				environment.CFrame.new(position) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2))
		end
		local angle = index / 4 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.4
		table.insert(drones, {parts = parts, offsets = offsets, eye = eye,
			hover = center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 5, 2.5 + index % 2 * 0.8, math.sin(angle) * 5),
			start = center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 30, 12, math.sin(angle) * 30), phase = math.random() * 6})
	end
	local function place(drone, position, elapsed)
		local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, environment.Vector3.new(center.X, position.Y, center.Z))
			* environment.CFrame.new(0, math.sin(elapsed * 6 + drone.phase) * 0.15, 0)
		local frames = {}
		for index, offset in drone.offsets do frames[index] = frame * offset end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, drone.parts, frames)
	end
	for _, drone in drones do place(drone, drone.start, 0) end
	local released = false
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.5, function(progress)
		for _, drone in drones do place(drone, drone.start:Lerp(drone.hover, 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 3), progress) end
	end, function()
		for index = 0, 3 do GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.08, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.servo, 0.4, 2.2) end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1, function(progress)
			for _, drone in drones do place(drone, drone.hover, 0.5 + progress) end
		end, function()
			for _, drone in drones do
				local finish = drone.hover + environment.Vector3.new(0, 25, 0) + (drone.hover - center) * 2
				GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.5, function(progress) place(drone, drone.hover:Lerp(finish, progress * progress), 1.5 + progress) end,
					function() for _, part in drone.parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.engine, 0.5, 1.6, nil, 1.5)
		end)
		for index = 1, 10 do
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.15 + index * 0.08, function()
				local drone = drones[(index - 1) % #drones + 1]
				if not drone.eye.Parent then return end
				local target = (body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or center)
					+ environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2
				local beams = {GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, drone.eye.Position, target, 0.1, white),
					GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, drone.eye.Position, target, 0.35, red, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.4)}
				for _, beam in beams do GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.12, {Transparency = 1}); GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.15) end
				GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, target, red, 6, 12, state.textures.spark)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, index % 2 == 0 and GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.laser1 or GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.laser3, 0.5, 2.2 + math.random() * 0.4)
				if body and not released then
					released = true
					body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), nil)
					return
				end
				if not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent then return end
				for _, part in body.body do
					pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity += environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0.6, math.random() - 0.5) * 10 end)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.engine, 0.5, 1.8, nil, 1.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.ufo(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 140), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)}
	local ceiling = context.workspace:Raycast(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), environment.Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), params)
	local altitude = ceiling and math.clamp(ceiling.Distance - 2.5, 6, 14) or 14
	local center = base + environment.Vector3.new(0, altitude, 0)
	local _, right = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local start = center + right * 40 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
	local parts, offsets = {}, {}
	local function create(shape, size, partColor, material, transparency, offset)
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Shape, part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = shape, true, false, false, false, true
		part.Size, part.Color, part.Material, part.Transparency = size, partColor, material, transparency or 0
		part.Reflectance, part.Parent = material == environment.Enum.Material.Metal and 0.3 or 0, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(parts, part)
		table.insert(offsets, offset)
		return part
	end
	local cylinder = environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
	create(environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Vector3.new(0.8, 8, 8), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 175, 185), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, 0, cylinder)
	create(environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Vector3.new(0.35, 8.6, 8.6), color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.2, environment.CFrame.new(0, -0.1, 0) * cylinder)
	create(environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, environment.Vector3.new(3.4, 3.4, 3.4), environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 230, 255), environment.Enum.Material.Glass, 0.35, environment.CFrame.new(0, 0.7, 0))
	local underside = create(environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 3, 3), color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0, environment.CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) * cylinder)
	local lamps = {}
	for index = 1, 8 do
		local angle = index / 8 * 6.283
		table.insert(lamps, create(environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, environment.Vector3.new(0.45, 0.45, 0.45), white, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0,
			environment.CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * 3.9, 0, math.sin(angle) * 3.9)))
	end
	local colors = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 80), environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 140), environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255)}
	local function place(position, elapsed)
		local frame = environment.CFrame.new(position) * environment.CFrame.Angles(math.sin(elapsed * 3) * 0.06, elapsed * 2, math.cos(elapsed * 3) * 0.06)
		local frames = {}
		for index, offset in offsets do frames[index] = frame * offset end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
		for index, lamp in lamps do lamp.Color = colors[(index + math.floor(elapsed * 8)) % #colors + 1] end
	end
	place(start, 0)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 1.5), 4.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.7, function(progress) place(start:Lerp(center, 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 3), progress * 0.7) end, function()
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(4, 0.3, 4))
		local first, second = environment.Instance.new("Attachment"), environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		first.Parent, second.Parent = underside, anchor
		local beam = GameEffects.Kill.attachmentBeam(state, first, second, {Width0 = 2.6, Width1 = 5.5, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color),
			Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.55, 1, 0.75), Segments = 1})
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, white),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.4, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(altitude / 9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 10),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(4, 4), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 40, true
		GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 4, 12, 1.6)
		local force, captured = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 1.3), false
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.5, function(progress)
			place(center, 0.7 + progress * 1.5)
			if not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent or captured then return end
			for _, part in body.body do
				local offset = part.Position - center
				local pull = environment.Vector3.new(-offset.X, 0, -offset.Z) * 2
				pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = pull + environment.Vector3.new(0, 5 + 7 * progress, 0) end)
			end
			if (body.torso.Position - center).Magnitude >= 2.2 then return end
			captured = true
			for _, part in body.parts do GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.15, {Transparency = 1}) end
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function() pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end) end)
		end, function()
			emitter.Enabled = false
			pcall(function() beam:Destroy() end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 0.6)
			if force then pcall(function() force:Destroy() end) end
			if body and not captured then pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end) end
			local finish = center - right * 60 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
