			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.45, function(progress) place(center:Lerp(finish, progress * progress), 2.2 + progress) end, function()
				local position = parts[1].Position
				for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
				local _, sparkle = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, sparkle, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
					Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0, 0.3, 8, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5),
					RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(160), Brightness = 8}, 1)
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic2, 0.6, 1.4, nil, 1.5)
		end)
		for index = 0, 2 do
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.12, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sciSynth, 0.4, 2, nil, 0.8) end)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonar, 0.7, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.engine, 0.5, 0.6, nil, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.execution(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local neck = effect.head - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0)
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local cameraRight = camera and camera.CFrame.RightVector or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(cameraRight.X, 0, cameraRight.Z)
	local right = horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 and horizontal.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local pivot, up = neck - right * 5.5, environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	local bladeColor, handleColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 20, 40), environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 18)
	local pieces = {}
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new(index < 3 and "WedgePart" or "Part")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
		part.Size = index < 3 and environment.Vector3.new(0.1, 6, 0.65) or environment.Vector3.new(0.25, 1.4, 0.3)
		part.Color, part.Material, part.Transparency = index < 3 and bladeColor or handleColor,
			index < 3 and environment.Enum.Material.Neon or environment.Enum.Material.Metal, index < 3 and 0.3 or 0
		part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(pieces, part)
	end
	local start = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(up, math.rad(-70)) * right
	GameEffects.Kill.arcFrom(state, pivot, start, up:Cross(start), 5.5, 0, math.rad(140), color, 0.9, 0.16, 0.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.16, function(progress)
		local direction = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(up, math.rad(-70 + 140 * progress)) * right
		local frame = environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(pivot, up, direction)
		pieces[1].CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 4, -1.3 / 4)
		pieces[2].CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) * environment.CFrame.new(0, 4, -1.3 / 4)
		pieces[3].CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0.4, 0)
	end, function()
		for _, part in pieces do
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 1})
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.3)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08, function()
		local outward = up:Cross(right)
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, -2, 0), function(result)
				if not result.head then return end
				for _, joint in result.model:GetDescendants() do
					if not joint:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then continue end
					if joint.Attachment0 and joint.Attachment0.Parent == result.head or joint.Attachment1 and joint.Attachment1.Parent == result.head then joint:Destroy() end
				end
				result.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = outward * 14 + right * 10 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
				result.head.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 12)
			end)
		end
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(neck), environment.Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4))
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 0, 25), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 10)),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.38, 1, 0.15), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(9, 15), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(20, 20), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -55, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 14)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 70, true
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.2, function(progress)
			if body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent then anchor.CFrame = body.torso.CFrame * environment.CFrame.new(0, body.torso.Size.Y / 2, 0) end
			emitter.Rate = 70 * (1 - progress)
		end, function() emitter.Enabled = false end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.8, function()
			local position = body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 4.5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.8, effect.ignore, 1.2)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bone2, 1, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.spurt, 0.8, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, neck, 0.8, 0.25)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(neck), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 14, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whipSlow, 1, 0.55)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.swordIn, 0.8, 0.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.missiles(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 1), 3.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local direction, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then origin = effect.killer.Position end
	local launch = origin + environment.Vector3.new(0, 25, 0) - direction * 10
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	for index = 1, 6 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.13, function()
			if not state.alive then return end
			local target = body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.chest
			if index % 2 == 0 then target += environment.Vector3.new(math.random() * 4 - 2, -1.5, math.random() * 4 - 2) end
			local start = launch + environment.Vector3.new(math.random() * 8 - 4, math.random() * 4, math.random() * 8 - 4)
			local control = (start + target) / 2 + environment.Vector3.new(math.random() * 10 - 5, 12, math.random() * 10 - 5)
			local missile = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			missile.Anchored, missile.CanCollide, missile.CanQuery, missile.CanTouch, missile.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
			missile.Size, missile.Material, missile.Color, missile.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.35, 0.35, 1.3), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 165), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			local tip = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			tip.Shape, tip.Anchored, tip.CanCollide, tip.CanQuery, tip.CanTouch = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false
			tip.Size, tip.Material, tip.Color, tip.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.38, 0.38, 0.38), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 50), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			local trail = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(start), environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3))
			local smoke = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, trail, {Texture = state.textures.soft,
				Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235), environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 155)), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 1.8),
				Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 1),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 0)
			smoke.Rate, smoke.Enabled = 70, true
			local flame = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, trail, {Texture = state.textures.flame,
				Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 20)), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.1),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.08, 0.14), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.1), Brightness = 4}, 0)
			flame.Rate, flame.Enabled = 60, true
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.5, function(progress)
				local position = GameEffects.Kill.bezier(start, control, target, progress)
				local ahead = GameEffects.Kill.bezier(start, control, target, math.min(1, progress + 0.02))
				local frame = (ahead - position).Magnitude > 0.001 and environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, ahead) or environment.CFrame.new(position)
				missile.CFrame, tip.CFrame, trail.CFrame = frame, frame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.7), frame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0, 0.7)
			end, function()
				pcall(function() missile:Destroy() end)
				pcall(function() tip:Destroy() end)
				smoke.Enabled, flame.Enabled = false, false
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, trail, 1)
				GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, target, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 40), 8, 5)
				GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, target, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 60), 16, 25)
				GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, target - environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 100, 95), 5, 6, 1.6)
				GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, target, 2.8, environment.Color3.fromRGB(25, 20, 18), 2.4, effect.ignore, 0.1, state.textures.burn, 1)
				if body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent then
					local offset = body.torso.Position - target
					local impact = offset.Magnitude > 0.1 and offset.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					for _, part in body.body do pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity += impact * 18 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 16, 0) end) end
				end
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, index % 2 == 0 and GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.explode or GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom, 0.6, 1 + math.random() * 0.3, trail)
				GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 0.7, 0.2)
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocket, 0.35, 1.3 + math.random() * 0.3, trail)
		end)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.bezier(start, control, finish, progress)
	return start * (1 - progress) ^ 2 + control * (2 * (1 - progress) * progress) + finish * progress ^ 2
end

function GameEffects.Kill.cannonball(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local fallback, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then origin = effect.killer.Position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0) end
	local target = effect.chest
	local offset = target - origin
	local direction = offset.Magnitude > 0.5 and offset.Unit or fallback
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local ball = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	ball.Shape, ball.Anchored, ball.CanCollide, ball.CanQuery, ball.CanTouch = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false
	ball.Size, ball.Material, ball.Color, ball.Reflectance = environment.Vector3.new(1.6, 1.6, 1.6), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34), 0.2
	local start = origin + direction * 2
	ball.CFrame, ball.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(start), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, ball, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220), environment.Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 145)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 1, 2), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 1), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 60, true
	GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, start, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200), 8, 6, 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, start, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 90), 6, 4)
	local control = (start + target) / 2 + environment.Vector3.new(0, offset.Magnitude * 0.12, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.35, function(progress)
		ball.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(GameEffects.Kill.bezier(start, control, target, progress))
	end, function()
		emitter.Enabled, ball.Anchored, ball.CanCollide = false, false, true
		pcall(function() ball.CollisionGroup = "Ragdolls" end)
		ball.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)
		local function launch() if ball.Parent then ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * 40 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 8, 0) end end
		launch()
		local connection
		connection = context.runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() connection:Disconnect(); launch() end)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.4, function() if ball.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ball, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ball, 2.9)
		if body then
			body.release(direction * 62 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 22, 0), nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10) end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, target, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 195, 190), 10, 8, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, target, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 120), 16, 20)
		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, target)
		local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 8),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Brightness = 3}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.metalHeavy, 1, 0.7)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bodyHeavy, 0.6, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bounce, 0.7, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 1.1, 0.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.cannon, 0.7, 1.3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.updateChain(state, chain, progress)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local offset = chain.part.Position - chain.origin
	local length = offset.Magnitude
	if length < 0.1 then return end
	local direction, count = offset / length, math.max(1, math.floor(length * progress / 0.42))
	for index = 1, math.max(count, #chain.links) do
		local link = chain.links[index]
		if not link and index <= count then
			link = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			link.Anchored, link.CanCollide, link.CanQuery, link.CanTouch, link.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
			link.Size, link.Material, link.Color, link.Reflectance = environment.Vector3.new(0.12, 0.3, 0.5), environment.Enum.Material.Metal, environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 78, 90), 0.25
			link.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			chain.links[index] = link
		end
		if not link then continue end
		if index > count then link.Transparency = 1; continue end
		local position = chain.origin + direction * (index - 0.5) * 0.42
		link.CFrame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, position + direction) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, index % 2 == 0 and math.pi / 2 or 0)
		link.Transparency = 0
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.chains(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if model then GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, color, 0.75, 0.3) end
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local names, chains = {}, {}
	for _, part in body do names[part.Name] = part end
	for index, name in {"LeftHand", "RightHand", "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "UpperTorso"} do
		local part = names[name]
		if not part then continue end
		local angle = index / 5 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.5
		local origin = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X + math.cos(angle) * 4.5, height, effect.root.Z + math.sin(angle) * 4.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, origin + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 2.2, color, 1.8, effect.ignore, 0.2, state.textures.ring, 1)
		table.insert(chains, {origin = origin, part = part, links = {}})
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.28, function(progress)
		for _, chain in chains do GameEffects.Kill.updateChain(state, chain, progress) end
	end, function()
		for index = 1, #chains do
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.03, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chainHit, 0.5, 1.2 + index * 0.1) end)
		end
	end)
	local frames = {}
	for index, part in body do frames[index] = part.CFrame end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.3, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.45, function()
			for index, part in body do
				if part.Parent then part.CFrame = frames[index] + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25, 0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25) end
			end
			for _, chain in chains do GameEffects.Kill.updateChain(state, chain, 1) end
		end, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.6, function(progress)
				local offset = environment.Vector3.new(0, -8 * progress * progress, 0)
				for index, part in body do if part.Parent then part.CFrame = frames[index] + offset end end
				for _, chain in chains do GameEffects.Kill.updateChain(state, chain, 1) end
			end, function()
				for _, chain in chains do for _, link in chain.links do pcall(function() link:Destroy() end) end end
				for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
				GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 12, 0.5, nil, effect.ignore)
				GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 40, 80), 8, 6, 2)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeDebris, 0.6, 0.8)
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chainRattle, 0.8, 0.7)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.growl, 0.6, 0.6)
		end)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 16, 1.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.demonHand(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local dark, red = environment.Color3.fromRGB(38, 6, 12), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 40)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local direction, up = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state), environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	local model = environment.Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name, model.Parent = "Hand", GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, red, red, 1, 0)
	local function create(size)
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, true
		part.Size, part.Material, part.Color, part.Parent = size, environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, dark, model
		return part
	end
	local palm = create(environment.Vector3.new(6, 1.2, 5.2))
	local fingers = {}
	for _, x in {-2.2, -0.75, 0.75, 2.2} do
		local segments = {}
		for _ = 1, 3 do table.insert(segments, create(environment.Vector3.new(1.05, 1.05, 1.9))) end
		table.insert(fingers, {x = x, z = 2.6, segments = segments, length = 1.9, yaw = 0})
	end
	table.insert(fingers, {x = -3.3, z = 0.3, segments = {create(environment.Vector3.new(1.15, 1.15, 1.7)), create(environment.Vector3.new(1.05, 1.05, 1.6))}, length = 1.65, yaw = math.rad(-35)})
	local function place(frame, curl)
		palm.CFrame = frame
		for _, finger in fingers do
			local joint = frame * environment.CFrame.new(finger.x, 0.3, finger.z) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, finger.yaw, 0)
			for _, segment in finger.segments do
				joint *= environment.CFrame.Angles(-curl, 0, 0)
				segment.CFrame = joint * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0, finger.length / 2)
				joint *= environment.CFrame.new(0, 0, finger.length)
			end
		end
	end
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.6, effect.root.Z) - direction * 1.2
	local function frame(position) return environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(position, up:Cross(direction), up) end
	place(frame(base - environment.Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)), 0.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.35, function(progress) place(frame(base - environment.Vector3.new(0, 8 * (1 - progress) ^ 2, 0)), 0.1) end, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, base, environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 60, 55), 10, 8, 2.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.3, function(progress) place(frame(base), 0.1 + 0.85 * progress) end, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.15, function(progress) place(frame(base), 0.95 + 0.2 * progress) end, function()
				for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
				local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
					Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
					Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.7, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(14, 28),
					SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.1}, 60)
				GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, effect.chest, red, 10, 4)
				GameEffects.Kill.splatRays(state, effect.chest, 10, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 12, effect.ignore, 1, 2.6, 3)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boneSquish, 1, 0.7, anchor)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocks, 0.7, 0.8, anchor)
				GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 1.1, 0.3)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.35, function()
					GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.6, function(progress) place(frame(base - environment.Vector3.new(0, 9 * progress * progress, 0)), 1.15) end,
						function() pcall(function() model:Destroy() end) end)
				end)
			end)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocks, 0.9, 0.6)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 7, environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 10, 10), 2.4, effect.ignore, 0.3, state.textures.cracks, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.roarDeep, 0.8, 0.4, nil, 2.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rise, 0.5, 0.4)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.anvil(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(62, 64, 70)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local rotation = environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.random() * 6.28, 0)
	local specs = {{"Part", environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 0.5, 1.4), environment.CFrame.new(0, 0.25, 0)},
		{"Part", environment.Vector3.new(1.2, 0.6, 0.9), environment.CFrame.new(0, 0.8, 0)},
		{"Part", environment.Vector3.new(2.8, 0.6, 1.3), environment.CFrame.new(0.3, 1.4, 0)},
		{"WedgePart", environment.Vector3.new(1.3, 0.6, 1.2), environment.CFrame.new(-1.7, 1.4, 0) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0)}}
	local pieces = {}
	for _, spec in specs do
		local part = environment.Instance.new(spec[1])
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, true
		part.Size, part.Color, part.Material, part.Reflectance, part.Parent = spec[2], color, environment.Enum.Material.Metal, 0.15, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(pieces, part)
	end
	local function place(y)
		local frame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, y, effect.root.Z) * rotation
		for index, part in pieces do part.CFrame = frame * specs[index][3] end
	end
	local top, bottom = effect.head.Y + 0.7, height + 0.45
	local start = top + 16
	place(start)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 3.5, environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.5, effect.ignore, 0.35, state.textures.soft, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.slideWhistle, 0.5, 0.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.32, function(progress) place(start + (top - start) * progress * progress) end, function()
		for _, part in body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local y = height + (part.Position.Y - height) * 0.16 + 0.1
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.08, {CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(part.Position.X, y, part.Position.Z) * part.CFrame.Rotation,
				Size = environment.Vector3.new(part.Size.X * 1.3, math.max(0.08, part.Size.Y * 0.25), part.Size.Z * 1.3)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.08, function(progress) place(top + (bottom - top) * progress) end, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.26, function(progress) place(bottom + math.sin(progress * math.pi) * 0.7) end)
			local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.2, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(3, 0.3, 3))
			local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
			attachment.Parent = anchor
			GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ringEdge, environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 215, 205), 2, 12, 0.45, 2)
			local dust = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 165, 158)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(dust, dust),
				Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 1.2, 1, 3.5), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.35, 1, 1),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 14), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85),
				Drag = 4, Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 16)
			local stars = {}
			for index = 1, 4 do
				local star = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, bottom + 2.6, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2))
				local starAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
				starAttachment.Parent = star
				local gold = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 120)
				local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, starAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(gold, gold),
					Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.55, 1, 0.55), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.12), LockedToPart = true, Brightness = 4, ZOffset = 1}, 0)
				emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 30, true
				table.insert(stars, {part = star, phase = index * math.pi / 2})
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.3, function(progress)
				local elapsed = progress * 1.3
				for _, star in stars do
					local angle = star.phase + elapsed * 7
					star.part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X + math.cos(angle) * 1.3, bottom + 2.6 + math.sin(elapsed * 9 + star.phase) * 0.12,
						effect.root.Z + math.sin(angle) * 1.3)
				end
			end, function() for _, star in stars do GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, star.part, 0) end end)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.anvil, 1, 0.8, anchor)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bounce, 0.7, 0.8, anchor)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.metalHeavy, 0.4, 1.4, anchor)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z), 0.9, 0.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1.5)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.9, function()
		for _, part in pieces do
			if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.45)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.4)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.spiritBomb(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 210, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.6, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)}
	local ceiling = context.workspace:Raycast(effect.chest, environment.Vector3.new(0, 30, 0), params)
	local height = ceiling and math.clamp(ceiling.Distance - 5.5, 6, 26) or 26
	local size = math.min(10, height * 0.8)
	local center = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, height, 0)
	local orb = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	orb.Shape, orb.Anchored, orb.CanCollide, orb.CanQuery, orb.CanTouch, orb.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	orb.Material, orb.Color, orb.Transparency, orb.Size, orb.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, 0.25, environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), environment.CFrame.new(center)
	orb.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local glow = orb:Clone()
	glow.Color, glow.Transparency, glow.Parent = white, 0.75, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(30, 30, 30))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0.2),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(20, 26), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere,
		ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Surface, ShapeInOut = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Inward,
		Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 4}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 120, true
	local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color, light.Range, light.Brightness, light.Shadows, light.Parent = color, 30, 2, false, orb
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.9, function(progress)
		local diameter = 0.5 + (size - 0.5) * progress
		orb.Size, glow.Size = environment.Vector3.new(diameter, diameter, diameter), environment.Vector3.new(diameter * 1.2, diameter * 1.2, diameter * 1.2)
		light.Brightness = 2 + 6 * progress
	end, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.4, function(progress)
			local position = center:Lerp(effect.chest, progress * progress)
			orb.CFrame, glow.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.CFrame.new(position)
		end, function()
			pcall(function() orb:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() glow:Destroy() end)
			GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, effect.chest, color, 26, 7)
			local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
			for index = 1, 3 do
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(index == 2 and white or color),
					Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 14 + index * 5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5 + index * 0.1), Brightness = 5}, 1)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.needles, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 6, 1, 16),
				Transparency = sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5}, 2)
			GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 40, 0.8, nil, effect.ignore)
			GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 180), 18, 14, 3)
			GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, effect.chest, 14, environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100), environment.Enum.Material.Slate, 0.4, 28, 1.6)
			if body then
				body.release(GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 25 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 50, 0), nil)
				pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10) end)
				local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, white, 0.3, 0)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function() if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.6, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end end)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.artillery, 1, 1.1, nil, 2.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.9, 0.8)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic2, 0.7, 0.8, nil, 1.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 2, 0.6)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.energyField, 0.8, 0.8, nil, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.5, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.energyField, 0.6, 1.1, nil, 1.4) end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.puppet(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 70)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), nil, 3.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if not body then return end
	local force = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 0.95)
	local center = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
	local forward, right = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local wood = environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 80, 45)
	local crossbar = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, center - right * 2.2, center + right * 2.2, 0.28, wood, environment.Enum.Material.Wood, 0)
	local lengthwise = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, center - forward * 1.6, center + forward * 1.6, 0.28, wood, environment.Enum.Material.Wood, 0)
	local offsets = {Head = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LeftHand = -right * 2.1, RightHand = right * 2.1, LeftFoot = -forward * 1.5, RightFoot = forward * 1.5}
	local drops = {Head = 5.2, LeftHand = 6.2, RightHand = 6.2, LeftFoot = 9.4, RightFoot = 9.4}
	local strings = {}
	for name, offset in offsets do
		local part = body.model:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not part then continue end
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center + offset), environment.Vector3.new(0.15, 0.15, 0.15))
		local first, second = environment.Instance.new("Attachment"), environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		first.Parent, second.Parent = anchor, part
		local beam = GameEffects.Kill.attachmentBeam(state, first, second, {Width0 = 0.05, Width1 = 0.05,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0, 1, 0), Segments = 2})
		table.insert(strings, {anchor = anchor, part = part, offset = offset, drop = drops[name], beam = beam})
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.5, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 1.5
		local rotation = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), math.sin(elapsed * 3) * 0.35)
		local position = center + environment.Vector3.new(math.sin(elapsed * 5) * 0.4, math.sin(elapsed * 7) * 0.3, 0)
		crossbar.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position) * rotation * (environment.CFrame.new(center):Inverse() * environment.CFrame.lookAt(center, center + right))
		lengthwise.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position) * rotation * (environment.CFrame.new(center):Inverse() * environment.CFrame.lookAt(center, center + forward))
		for _, string in strings do
			local top = position + rotation * string.offset
			string.anchor.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(top)
			local target = top - environment.Vector3.new(0, string.drop - math.sin(elapsed * 14 + string.drop) * 1.2, 0)
			if string.part.Parent and string.beam.Parent then pcall(function() string.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (target - string.part.Position) * 7 end) end
		end
	end, function()
		if force then force:Destroy() end
		for _, string in strings do
			if string.part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, string.part.Position, color, 6, 12, state.textures.spark) end
			pcall(function() string.beam:Destroy() end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, string.anchor, 0)
		end
		for _, part in {crossbar, lengthwise} do
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Transparency = 1})
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.45)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.snip, 1, 1.3)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rip, 0.8, 1.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function()
			local position = body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 3.5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.4, effect.ignore, 0.9)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whisper, 0.4, 1.7)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.servo, 0.5, 0.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.jackpot(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2, 4, 1.4))
	if model then
		local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 180), 1, 0)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.25, {FillTransparency = 0.1})
	end
	local sizes = {}
	for index, part in body do sizes[index] = part.Size end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.3, function(progress)
		for index, part in body do if part.Parent then part.Size = sizes[index] * (1 + 0.15 * progress) end end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.3, function()
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		if model then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end) end
		local coins = {}
		for _ = 1, 24 do
			local coin = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			coin.Shape, coin.Size = environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Vector3.new(0.14, 0.85, 0.85)
			coin.Material, coin.Color, coin.Reflectance = environment.Enum.Material.Foil, color:Lerp(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 140), math.random()), 0.25
			coin.CanQuery, coin.CanTouch = false, false
			pcall(function() coin.CollisionGroup = "Ragdolls" end)
			local offset = environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 1.4, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2.6, math.random() - 0.5)
			coin.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest + offset) * environment.CFrame.Angles(math.random() * 6.28, math.random() * 6.28, math.random() * 6.28)
			coin.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)
			table.insert(coins, coin)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.4 + math.random() * 0.4, function() if coin.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, coin, 0.4, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, coin, 3.2)
		end
		local function launch()
			for _, coin in coins do
				if not coin.Parent then continue end
				local offset = coin.Position - effect.chest
				local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z)
				local direction = horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 and horizontal.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
				coin.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * (6 + math.random() * 8) + environment.Vector3.new(0, 14 + math.random() * 12, 0)
				coin.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 15)
			end
		end
		launch()
		local connection
		connection = context.runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() connection:Disconnect(); launch() end)
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent = anchor
		local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
			Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 1.2, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 14),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Brightness = 6}, 18)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 7), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35), Brightness = 3}, 1)
		for _ = 1, 9 do
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.45 + math.random() * 1.1, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.coinDrop, 0.3, 1.8 + math.random() * 0.8, anchor) end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.cash, 1, 1, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 7, 16, 0.7)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.crows(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(14, 12, 18)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, color = color, transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 4.6, 1.4))
	for _, part in body do
		local frame = part.CFrame
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.35, function(progress)
			if part.Parent then part.CFrame = frame + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.12 * progress, 0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.12 * progress) end
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.crow, 0.9, 1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.35, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.2)
		local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
		local colors = function(value) return environment.ColorSequence.new(value, value) end
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.leaf, Color = colors(color), Size = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0.3),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 9), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Drag = 2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -3, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300),
			Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 28)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
			FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(24, 20, 30)), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 5),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 0.7, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 10)
		local birds, parts = {}, {}
		local function createPart(size)
			local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
			part.Size, part.Color, part.Material, part.Parent = size, color, environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			table.insert(parts, part)
			return part
		end
		for _ = 1, 16 do
			local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0, math.random() - 0.5)
			horizontal = (horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 and horizontal.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)) + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.55 + math.random() * 0.45, 0)
			local position = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6, (math.random() - 0.5) * 3, (math.random() - 0.5))
			table.insert(birds, {position = position, velocity = horizontal.Unit * (11 + math.random() * 8), phase = math.random() * 6.28,
				turn = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2.4, body = createPart(environment.Vector3.new(0.4, 0.32, 0.85)),
				left = createPart(environment.Vector3.new(0.85, 0.05, 0.45)), right = createPart(environment.Vector3.new(0.85, 0.05, 0.45))})
		end
		local previous = 0
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.9, function(progress)
			local elapsed = progress * 1.9
			local delta = elapsed - previous
			previous = elapsed
			local fade = math.clamp((elapsed - 1.5) / 0.4, 0, 1)
			local frames = {}
			for _, bird in birds do
				bird.velocity = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), bird.turn * delta) * bird.velocity + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3 * delta, 0)
				bird.position += bird.velocity * delta
				local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(bird.position, bird.position + bird.velocity)
				local flap = math.sin(elapsed * 26 + bird.phase) * 0.9
				table.insert(frames, frame)
				table.insert(frames, frame * environment.CFrame.new(-0.18, 0.05, 0) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, flap) * environment.CFrame.new(-0.42, 0, 0))
				table.insert(frames, frame * environment.CFrame.new(0.18, 0.05, 0) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -flap) * environment.CFrame.new(0.42, 0, 0))
				if fade > 0 then bird.body.Transparency, bird.left.Transparency, bird.right.Transparency = fade, fade, fade end
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
		end, function() for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.crow, 0.8, 1.15, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.25, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.crow, 0.7, 0.9, anchor) end)
		for index = 0, 5 do
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.09, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.batWings, 0.4, 1.6 + math.random() * 0.5, anchor) end)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 200), 2, 10, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.attachmentBeam(state, first, second, properties)
	local beam = state.context.environment.Instance.new("Beam")
	beam.Attachment0, beam.Attachment1, beam.FaceCamera, beam.LightEmission, beam.LightInfluence, beam.Segments = first, second, true, 1, 0, 12
	for key, value in properties do beam[key] = value end
	beam.Parent = first.Parent
	return beam
end

function GameEffects.Kill.disco(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local colors = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 200), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 60),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 40), environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 90, 255)}
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)}
	local ceiling = context.workspace:Raycast(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), environment.Vector3.new(0, 14, 0), params)
	local center = base + environment.Vector3.new(0, ceiling and math.clamp(ceiling.Distance - 1.5, 5, 10) or 10, 0)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), nil, 3.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local force = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 0.98)
	local ball = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	ball.Shape, ball.Anchored, ball.CanCollide, ball.CanQuery, ball.CanTouch = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false
	ball.Size, ball.Material, ball.Color = environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 2.2, 2.2), environment.Enum.Material.Foil, environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 225, 235)
	ball.Reflectance, ball.CFrame, ball.Parent = 0.6, environment.CFrame.new(center + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colorSequence = function(color) return environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, ball, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colorSequence(white), Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.5, 0.8, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, Brightness = 6, ZOffset = 1}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 25, true
	local source = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	source.Parent = ball
	local spots = {}
	for index, color in colors do
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2))
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent = anchor
		local beam = GameEffects.Kill.attachmentBeam(state, source, attachment, {Width0 = 0.25, Width1 = 1.6, Color = colorSequence(color), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0.6), Segments = 1})
		local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
		light.Color, light.Range, light.Brightness, light.Shadows, light.Parent = color, 6, 2, false, anchor
		table.insert(spots, {anchor = anchor, beam = beam, phase = index / #colors * 6.283, radius = 3 + index % 3})
	end
	local highlight = body and GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, colors[1], white, 0.5, 0.2)
	local nextBounce = 0
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 2.1, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 2.1
		local lowered = math.min(1, elapsed / 0.3)
		ball.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3 * (1 - lowered), 0)) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 3, 0)
		for _, spot in spots do
			local angle = spot.phase + elapsed * 2.5
			spot.anchor.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * spot.radius, 0.2, math.sin(angle) * spot.radius))
		end
		if highlight and highlight.Parent then highlight.FillColor = colors[math.floor(elapsed * 7) % #colors + 1] end
		if elapsed < nextBounce or not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent then return end
		nextBounce = elapsed + 0.25
		for _, part in body.body do
			pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity += environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() * 0.8, math.random() - 0.5) * 9 end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bounce, 0.35, 1 + math.floor(elapsed * 4) % 2 * 0.3)
	end, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		if force then pcall(function() force:Destroy() end) end
		for _, spot in spots do pcall(function() spot.beam:Destroy() end); GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, spot.anchor, 0) end
		if highlight then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.4, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ball, 0.4, {CFrame = ball.CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Transparency = 1})
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ball, 0.45)
		local _, burst = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
		for _, color in colors do
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colorSequence(color), Size = sequence(0, 0.22, 1, 0.18),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2, 2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 18), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
				Drag = 2.5, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -14, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-400, 400),
				LightEmission = 0.2, LightInfluence = 0.6, Brightness = 1.6}, 10)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.6, 1.4, nil, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.partyHorn2, 0.5, 1.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.partyHorn2, 0.5, 0.9)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.hollowPurple(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local blue, red, purple = environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 130, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 60, 255)
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local side = environment.Vector3.new(-direction.Z, 0, direction.X)
	local center = effect.chest - direction * 7
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local function makeOrb(color)
		local orb = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		orb.Shape, orb.Anchored, orb.CanCollide, orb.CanQuery, orb.CanTouch, orb.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
		orb.Material, orb.Color, orb.Size, orb.Parent = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, environment.Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		local glow = orb:Clone()
		glow.Transparency, glow.Size, glow.Parent = 0.65, environment.Vector3.new(2.6, 2.6, 2.6), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, orb, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 7), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 50, true
		local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
		light.Color, light.Range, light.Brightness, light.Shadows, light.Parent = color, 12, 4, false, orb
		return {orb, glow}
	end
	local first, second = makeOrb(blue), makeOrb(red)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.55, function(progress)
		local eased = progress * progress
		local rotation = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(direction, eased * 3)
		local offset = rotation * side * 6 * (1 - eased)
		first[1].CFrame, first[2].CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center + offset), environment.CFrame.new(center + offset)
		second[1].CFrame, second[2].CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center - offset), environment.CFrame.new(center - offset)
	end, function()
		for _, group in {first, second} do for _, part in group do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end
		local orb = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		orb.Shape, orb.Anchored, orb.CanCollide, orb.CanQuery, orb.CanTouch, orb.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
		orb.Material, orb.Color, orb.Size, orb.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, purple, environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), environment.CFrame.new(center)
		orb.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		local glow = orb:Clone()
		glow.Color, glow.Transparency, glow.Parent = environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 20, 160), 0.5, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
		light.Color, light.Range, light.Brightness, light.Shadows, light.Parent = purple, 26, 8, false, orb
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, orb, 0.18, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(4.5, 4.5, 4.5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.18, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(6, 6, 6)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, purple, 12, 5)
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, orb, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(purple, environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 0, 120)),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 0.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, Brightness = 3}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 60, true
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic, 0.8, 0.8, nil, 1.5)
		local hit = false
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.6, function(progress)
				local position = center + direction * 55 * progress
				orb.CFrame, glow.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.CFrame.new(position)
				if hit or (position - effect.chest).Magnitude >= 3.5 then return end
				hit = true
				for _, part in body do
					if not part.Parent then continue end
					part.Material, part.Color = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, purple
					GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.15, {Transparency = 1, Size = part.Size * 0.4})
					GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.2)
				end
				local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(white, purple), Size = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 0),
					Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 18), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Drag = 2, Brightness = 4}, 50)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.8, 0.9)
				GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 1.6, 0.4)
			end, function()
				emitter.Enabled = false
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, orb, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(8, 8, 8), Transparency = 1})
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(10, 10, 10), Transparency = 1})
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, orb, 0.3)
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, glow, 0.3)
			end)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6, environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 10, 90), 2.4, effect.ignore, 0.3, state.textures.burn, 2)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.powerUp, 0.7, 0.9, nil, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicGrowl, 0.6, 0.8)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.blackFlash(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local red, black = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 40), environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	local direction, center = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect), effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local core = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	core.Shape, core.Anchored, core.CanCollide, core.CanQuery, core.CanTouch, core.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	core.Material, core.Color, core.Transparency = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, black, 0.1
	core.Size, core.CFrame, core.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), environment.CFrame.new(center), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local glow = core:Clone()
	glow.Color, glow.Transparency, glow.Parent = red, 0.55, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, core, 0.12, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(5, 5, 5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.14, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(6, 6, 6)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.13, function()
		if core.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, core, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2), Transparency = 1}) end
		if glow.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(9, 9, 9), Transparency = 1}) end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, core, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, glow, 0.5)
	local bolts = {}
	local function clear()
		for _, part in bolts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		table.clear(bolts)
	end
	local function refresh()
		clear()
		for _ = 1, 7 do
			local offset = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.4, math.random() - 0.5).Unit * (4 + math.random() * 4)
			local parts = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, center, center + offset, red, 0.2, 0.9, 6, black)
			for _, part in parts do table.insert(bolts, part) end
		end
	end
	refresh()
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.06, refresh)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.13, refresh)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.22, clear)
	GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, center, red, 40, 45, state.textures.spark)
	local up = math.abs(direction.Y) < 0.95 and environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	for index = 1, 3 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.05, function()
			local frame = environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(center + direction * (index - 1) * 1.2, direction:Cross(up).Unit, direction)
			local ring = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, frame, 0.5, index == 2 and black or red, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.2)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ring, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.04, 9 - index, 9 - index), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ring, 0.35)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.05, function()
		if not body then return end
		body.release(direction * 75 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), nil)
		pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10) end)
		local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, black, red, 0.2, 0)
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			for index = 1, 8 do
				if not highlight.Parent then return end
				highlight.FillColor = index % 2 == 0 and red or black
				environment.task.wait(0.06 / state.speed)
			end
			if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.4, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
		end)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, red, 12, 30, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.punch, 1, 1.3)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bone1, 1, 0.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.6, 1.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 2, 0.4)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.cubeCorners(state, frame, size)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local half, corners = size / 2, {}
	for index = 0, 7 do
		local x = index % 2 == 1 and half or -half
		local y = math.floor(index / 2) % 2 == 1 and half or -half
		local z = math.floor(index / 4) % 2 == 1 and half or -half
		table.insert(corners, frame * environment.Vector3.new(x, y, z))
	end
	return corners
end

function GameEffects.Kill.updateSegment(state, part, start, finish, width)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local offset = finish - start
	local length = offset.Magnitude
	if length < 0.01 then part.Transparency = 1; return end
	local up = math.abs(offset.Unit.Y) > 0.98 and environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) or environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	part.Size, part.CFrame = environment.Vector3.new(width, width, length), environment.CFrame.lookAt((start + finish) / 2, finish, up)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.tesseract(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local center, segments = effect.chest, {}
	for index = 1, 32 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
		part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, index > 24 and environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 120, 255) or color, index > 24 and 0.35 or 0
		part.Size, part.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(segments, part)
	end
	local edges = {{1, 2}, {2, 4}, {4, 3}, {3, 1}, {5, 6}, {6, 8}, {8, 7}, {7, 5}, {1, 5}, {2, 6}, {3, 7}, {4, 8}}
	local frames = {}
	for index, part in body do frames[index] = {frame = part.CFrame, size = part.Size} end
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.1, function(progress)
		local eased, size = progress * progress, 6.5 * (1 - 0.92 * progress * progress)
		local outer = GameEffects.Kill.cubeCorners(state, environment.CFrame.new(center) * environment.CFrame.Angles(progress * 2.5, progress * 4, progress * 1.5), size)
		local inner = GameEffects.Kill.cubeCorners(state, environment.CFrame.new(center) * environment.CFrame.Angles(-progress * 3, -progress * 5, progress * 2), size * 0.5)
		for index, edge in edges do
			GameEffects.Kill.updateSegment(state, segments[index], outer[edge[1]], outer[edge[2]], 0.14)
			GameEffects.Kill.updateSegment(state, segments[12 + index], inner[edge[1]], inner[edge[2]], 0.1)
		end
		for index = 1, 8 do GameEffects.Kill.updateSegment(state, segments[24 + index], outer[index], inner[index], 0.06) end
		for index, part in body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local original = frames[index]
			part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center + (original.frame.Position - center) * (1 - eased)) * original.frame.Rotation * environment.CFrame.Angles(eased * 3, eased * 2, 0)
			part.Size = original.size * (1 - 0.8 * eased)
		end
	end, function()
		for _, group in {segments, body} do for _, part in group do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 10, 5)
		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 22), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Brightness = 5}, 50)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(color), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 12),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.45), Brightness = 5}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.synthSpark, 0.7, 1.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sciSynth, 0.7, 0.8, nil, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.7, 0.25)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 14, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.powerUp, 0.6, 1.3, nil, 1.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.servo, 0.4, 1.2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.nuke(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color = white, transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	for _, part in body do GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 20, 15)}) end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.25, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.3, function(part) return {CFrame = part.CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)} end)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 200), 20, 60, 1.4)
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 150)),
		Size = sequence(0, 4, 1, 30), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 8, ZOffset = 2}, 2)
	local fire = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	fire.Shape, fire.Anchored, fire.CanCollide, fire.CanQuery, fire.CanTouch, fire.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	local bright, dark = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 110), environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 50, 15)
	fire.Material, fire.Color, fire.Size, fire.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, bright, environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
	fire.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local cloud = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(4, 3, 4))
	local cloudEmitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, cloud, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 110, 50), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 40, 35)),
		Size = sequence(0, 4, 1, 10), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4, 2.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 7),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 60), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-30, 30),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.25, LightInfluence = 0.6, Brightness = 1.3}, 0)
	cloudEmitter.Rate, cloudEmitter.Enabled = 45, true
	local column = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(2.2, 10, 2.2))
	local columnEmitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, column, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 110, 90), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 50, 45)),
		Size = sequence(0, 2.5, 1, 5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 8),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(10, 10), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		LightEmission = 0.1, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.1}, 0)
	columnEmitter.Rate, columnEmitter.Enabled = 55, true
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.6, function(progress)
		local eased = 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 2
		local center = position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2 + 11 * eased, 0)
		fire.CFrame, cloud.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.CFrame.new(center)
		fire.Size = environment.Vector3.new(1 + 6.5 * eased, 1 + 5.5 * eased, 1 + 6.5 * eased)
		fire.Color, fire.Transparency = bright:Lerp(dark, progress), progress > 0.7 and (progress - 0.7) / 0.3 or 0
	end, function()
		cloudEmitter.Enabled, columnEmitter.Enabled = false, false
		pcall(function() fire:Destroy() end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, cloud, 2.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, column, 2)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, white, 2, 45, 0.8, state.textures.ringEdge, effect.ignore)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.25, function()
		local ringAnchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4))
		local ringAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		ringAttachment.Parent = ringAnchor
		GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, ringAttachment, state.textures.ring, white, 2, 28, 0.7, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ringAnchor, 1.5)
	end)
	local smoke = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(14, 1, 14))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, smoke, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
		FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 160, 150)),
		Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 7), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.25, 0.7, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2, 1.8),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 16), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85), Drag = 2, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 30)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, smoke, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 16, environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 85, 80), environment.Enum.Material.Slate, 0.5, 30, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 14, environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 14), 3, effect.ignore, 0.2, state.textures.burn, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quake, 1, 0.55, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.artillery, 0.9, 0.5, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeRumble, 0.7, 0.6, anchor, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 2.6, 0.9)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.shadowClones(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 60, 255)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local source = effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent
	if not source or not source:IsA("Model") or not source:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then source = effect.char end
	local root = source:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or source:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	local center, clones, angle = effect.chest, {}, math.random() * 6.28
	for index = 1, 5 do
		local parts = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, source, {material = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(16, 10, 26), transparency = 1})
		local rootFrame = root and root.CFrame or environment.CFrame.new(center)
		local offsets = {}
		for partIndex, part in parts do offsets[partIndex] = rootFrame:Inverse() * part.CFrame end
		local phase = angle + (index - 1) * 1.2566
		local position = environment.Vector3.new(center.X + math.cos(phase) * 7, effect.root.Y, center.Z + math.sin(phase) * 7)
		local clone = {parts = parts, offsets = offsets, position = position}
		table.insert(clones, clone)
		local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, environment.Vector3.new(center.X, position.Y, center.Z))
		local targets = {}
		for partIndex, offset in offsets do targets[partIndex] = frame * offset end
		GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, targets)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.05, function()
			for _, part in parts do GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.2, {Transparency = 0.15}) end
			GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, position - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 60), 4, 3, 2)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicZoom, 0.8, 0.6)
	for index, clone in clones do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4 + (index - 1) * 0.11, function()
			local start = clone.position
			local finish = environment.Vector3.new(2 * center.X - start.X, start.Y, 2 * center.Z - start.Z)
			local beam = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), finish + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 0.25, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.35, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.02, 0.02, beam.Size.Z)})
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.4)
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.1, function(progress)
				local position = start:Lerp(finish, progress)
				local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, position + (finish - start))
				local targets = {}
				for partIndex, offset in clone.offsets do targets[partIndex] = frame * offset end
				GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, clone.parts, targets)
			end, function()
				for _, part in clone.parts do
					GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 1})
					GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.3)
				end
				GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, finish - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 60), 4, 3, 2)
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, center + environment.Vector3.new(0, (index - 3) * 0.3, 0), math.random(0, 180), 3.2, 1, color, 0.6, 0.05, 0.08, math.random(-30, 30))
			GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, center, color, 10, 20, state.textures.spark)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshQuick, 0.7, 1 + index * 0.1)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function()
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 45, 0), nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10) end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 12, 5)
		GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 16, 0.5, nil, effect.ignore)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fleshSlash, 0.9, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.punch, 0.6, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 1, 0.3)
