		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2, function()
			local position = body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent and body.torso.Position or effect.root
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 4, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 2.4, effect.ignore, 1)
		end)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.orbital(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local blue, red, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(3, 0.3, 3))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	for _, data in {{state.textures.dial, 9, 3, 260}, {state.textures.reticle, 5, 2, -180}} do
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = data[1], Color = colors(red), Size = sequence(0, data[2], 1, data[3]),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 0.8, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.01),
			EmissionDirection = environment.Enum.NormalId.Top, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular,
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(data[4]), Brightness = 4}, 1)
	end
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	local glow = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 240, 255), transparency = 1})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	for index = 0, 2 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.11, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.beep, 0.5, 1.5, anchor) end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.charge, 0.7, 1.3, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.36, function()
		if not state.alive then return end
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for _, part in glow do
			part.Transparency = 0
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.35, {CFrame = part.CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), Transparency = 1, Size = part.Size * 0.4},
				environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.4)
		end
		local beam = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		beam.Shape, beam.Anchored, beam.CanCollide, beam.CanQuery, beam.CanTouch, beam.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, true, false, false, false, false
		beam.Material, beam.Color, beam.Size = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, white, environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.2, 0.2)
		beam.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 80, 0)) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
		beam.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		local outer = beam:Clone()
		outer.Color, outer.Transparency, outer.Size, outer.Parent = blue, 0.55, environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.4, 0.4), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.08, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 2.6, 2.6)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, outer, 0.1, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 5.5, 5.5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.42, function()
			if beam.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.05, 0.05), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
			if outer.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, outer, 0.35, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(160, 0.1, 0.1), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.9)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, outer, 0.9)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = colors(white, blue), Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 14),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.45), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5, ZOffset = 1}, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ringEdge, blue, 3, 26, 0.7, 4)
		GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ring, white, 2, 16, 0.4, 5)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.fsparks, Color = colors(white, blue), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(25, 45), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(75, 75),
			Drag = 2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -45, 0), Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 45)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 66, 62)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0.35), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(14, 24),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(40, 40), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 22)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 160, 170), environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 64, 70)),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 8), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2, 1.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 6),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-40, 40), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 12, 0.15)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 7.5, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40), 0.9, effect.ignore, 0.12, state.textures.ringEdge, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 7, environment.Color3.fromRGB(18, 16, 16), 2.6, effect.ignore, 0.12, state.textures.burn, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, blue, 14, 50, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom2, 1, 1, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.7, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, base, 2.2, 0.55)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 4.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.laser(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local direction, right = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local top = effect.head.Y + 0.8
	local cuts = {height + (top - height) * 0.28, height + (top - height) * 0.52, height + (top - height) * 0.76}
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.15, 0.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 16),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 5, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 0)
	for index, y in cuts do
		local side = index % 2 == 0 and -1 or 1
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.13, function()
			local origin = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			local core = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, origin, direction * 14, 0.07, white)
			local glow = GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, origin, direction * 14, 0.3, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.45)
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.14, function(progress)
				local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, y, effect.root.Z) + right * (-3.5 + 7 * progress) * side
				core.CFrame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, position + direction)
				glow.CFrame, anchor.CFrame = core.CFrame, environment.CFrame.new(position)
				if math.abs(progress - 0.5) < 0.3 then emitter:Emit(3) end
			end, function()
				for _, beam in {core, glow} do
					GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.12, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, beam.Size.Z)})
					GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.15)
				end
			end)
			local ring = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, y, effect.root.Z), 2.6, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.4)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ring, 0.7, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.04, 3.2, 3.2)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ring, 0.75)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.laser1, 0.6, 2 + index * 0.15, anchor)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.laser2, 0.3, 2)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.48, function()
		local levels, shifts, axes = {height, cuts[1], cuts[2], cuts[3]}, {0, 0.5, -0.8, 1.2}, {}
		for index = 1, 4 do axes[index] = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0, math.random() - 0.5).Unit end
		for _, part in body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local section = 1
			for index, y in cuts do if part.Position.Y > y then section = index + 1 end end
			local shift = right * shifts[section]
			local fall = levels[section] - height
			local pivot = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, levels[section], effect.root.Z)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.16, {CFrame = part.CFrame + shift}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function()
				if not part.Parent then return end
				local rotation = environment.CFrame.new(pivot + shift) * environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(axes[section], 0.5 + section * 0.15) * environment.CFrame.new(-(pivot + shift))
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.45, {CFrame = rotation * part.CFrame - environment.Vector3.new(0, fall * 0.9, 0)},
					environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function() GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.4) end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sear, 0.5, 1.4, anchor)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 12, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.voidRift(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local center = effect.chest + direction * 2.4
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local roll = math.random(-20, 20)
	local frame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(center, center - direction) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(roll))
	local parts = {}
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
		part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, index == 1 and environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or color, 0
		part.Size, part.CFrame, part.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 7, 0.1), frame, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(parts, part)
	end
	local function resize(width)
		parts[1].Size = environment.Vector3.new(math.max(0.05, width), 7 * math.min(1, 0.3 + width), 0.1)
		parts[2].Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.12, parts[1].Size.Y + 0.2, 0.14)
		parts[3].Size = parts[2].Size
		parts[2].CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.new(-width / 2, 0, -0.02)
		parts[3].CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.new(width / 2, 0, -0.02)
	end
	resize(0.05)
	GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, center, 90 + roll, 5, 0.6, color, 0.8, 0.08, 0.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.35, function(progress) resize(2.4 * (1 - (1 - progress) ^ 3)) end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(10, 10, 10))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0.1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35, 0.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(12, 18),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Surface,
		ShapeInOut = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Inward, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 4}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 90, true
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 18, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.35, function()
		if body then body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), nil) end
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.9, function(progress)
			if not body then return end
			for _, part in body.parts do
				if not part.Parent or part.Transparency >= 1 then continue end
				local offset = center - part.Position
				if offset.Magnitude < 1.3 then
					part.Transparency = 1
					for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then child.Transparency = 1 end end
					continue
				end
				if not part.Massless then pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = offset.Unit * (14 + 34 * progress) end) end
			end
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.3, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.15, function(progress) resize(2.4 * (1 - progress)) end, function()
			for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
			if body then pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end) end
			GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 12, 5)
			GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 14, 0.5, nil, effect.ignore)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic2, 0.7, 1.4, nil, 1.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.8, 0.25)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rip, 0.7, 0.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.suck, 0.8, 0.7)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.vortexBlast, 0.5, 0.8)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.levitate(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 110, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 1.2), 3.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local force = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 1.03)
	if body and body.torso then GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, color, 0.75, 0.2) end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.2, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(6, 0.4, 6))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local rocks = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 94, 88)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4, 2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1.5, 3),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(15, 15), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-90, 90), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 6)
	rocks.Rate, rocks.Enabled = 14, true
	local glow = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(color, white), Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1.5, 3), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(20, 20),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 0)
	glow.Rate, glow.Enabled = 22, true
	GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ring, color, 2, 8, 1.2, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.forceField, 0.5, 0.7, anchor, 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicGlow, 0.3, 1.8, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.45, function()
		rocks.Enabled, glow.Enabled = false, false
		if force then force:Destroy() end
		if not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent then return end
		for _, part in body.body do pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(0, -95, 0) end) end
		local landed = false
		local function impact(position)
			if landed then return end
			landed = true
			local burst = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position.X, height + 0.2, position.Z), environment.Vector3.new(3, 0.4, 3))
			local burstAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
			burstAttachment.Parent = burst
			GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, burstAttachment, state.textures.ringEdge, color, 2, 16, 0.5, 4)
			GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, burstAttachment, state.textures.cracks, color, 5, 6, 1, 3)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 145, 140)),
				Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 4.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1),
				Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 14), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85), Drag = 4,
				Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 16)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 84, 78)),
				Size = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 18),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(40, 40), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
				RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 20)
			GameEffects.Kill.light(state, burst, color, 8, 22, 0.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quake2, 1, 0.9, burst, 1.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom2, 0.5, 0.9, burst)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 1.2, 0.35)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, burst, 2)
		end
		local position = body.torso.Position
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.7, function(progress)
			if not body.torso.Parent then return end
			position = body.torso.Position
			if progress > 0.05 and body.torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > -25 then impact(position) end
		end, function() impact(position) end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.matrix(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local green, gold, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 120), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local fallback, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then origin = effect.killer.Position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0) end
	local target = effect.head
	local offset = target - origin
	local direction = offset.Magnitude > 0.5 and offset.Unit or fallback
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.8, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local rain = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(12, 1, 12))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, rain, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 255, 200), green),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.16, 1, 0.1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(14, 22),
		EmissionDirection = environment.Enum.NormalId.Bottom, Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 140, true
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.6, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, rain, 2.8)
	local start = target - direction * 12
	local bullet = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	bullet.Anchored, bullet.CanCollide, bullet.CanQuery, bullet.CanTouch, bullet.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
	bullet.Size, bullet.Material, bullet.Color = environment.Vector3.new(0.14, 0.14, 0.45), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, gold
	bullet.CFrame, bullet.Parent = environment.CFrame.lookAt(start, target), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local nextRing = 0
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1, function(progress)
		local position = start:Lerp(target, progress)
		bullet.CFrame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, position + direction)
		if progress < nextRing then return end
		nextRing = progress + 0.11
		local ring = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position, direction), 0.3, white, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ring, 0.5, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.03, 2.6, 2.6), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ring, 0.55)
	end, function()
		pcall(function() bullet:Destroy() end)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, target, gold, 6, 4)
		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, target)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 18), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0.15), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 7),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(50, 50), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -12, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-60, 60), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 20)
		if body then
			body.release(direction * 22 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), function(result)
				if result.head then result.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * 30 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0) end
			end)
			local force = GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 0.85)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2, function() if force then pcall(function() force:Destroy() end) end end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sniper, 0.9, 0.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.snap, 0.8, 0.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 0.6, 0.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.suck, 0.7, 0.35)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sciSynth, 0.3, 0.6, nil, 0.8)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.thunder(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local impact = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
	local start = base + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 10, 80, (math.random() - 0.5) * 10)
	local bolts = {}
	local function refresh(width)
		for _, part in bolts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		local points
		bolts, points = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, start, impact, color, width, 3.2, 16)
		for _ = 1, 2 do
			local point = points[math.random(4, #points - 4)]
			local destination = point + environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 16, -(6 + math.random() * 10), (math.random() - 0.5) * 16)
			local parts = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, point, destination, color, width * 0.55, 1.6, 6)
			for _, part in parts do table.insert(bolts, part) end
		end
		local parts = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, impact, base, color, width, 0.6, 4)
		for _, part in parts do table.insert(bolts, part) end
	end
	refresh(0.32)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.07, function() if state.alive then refresh(0.24) end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.15, function() if state.alive then refresh(0.36) end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.26, function()
		local old = bolts
		bolts = {}
		for _, part in old do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.02, 0.02, part.Size.Z)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.3)
		end
	end)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(210, 240, 255), transparency = 0.1})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		for index = 1, 6 do
			for _, part in body do if part.Parent then part.Transparency = index % 2 == 0 and 0.1 or 0.75 end end
			environment.task.wait(0.05 / state.speed)
		end
		for _, part in body do if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.3, {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42), Transparency = 0}) end end
		environment.task.wait(0.35 / state.speed)
		GameEffects.Kill.fadeGhost(state, body, 0.6, function(part) return {CFrame = part.CFrame + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)} end)
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(2, 0.4, 2))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 10),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5, ZOffset = 1}, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ringEdge, color, 2, 18, 0.5, 4)
	GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.cracks, color, 5, 6.5, 0.9, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.fsparks, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(20, 40), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(70, 70),
		Drag = 2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -50, 0), Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 40)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 66)), Size = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0.3),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(12, 20), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(45, 45),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300),
		LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 16)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 95, 105), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 42, 48)),
		Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 6), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 4),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(40, 40), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-40, 40), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 8, 0.25)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6, environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24), 2.6, effect.ignore, 0.15, state.textures.burn, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 255), 14, 50, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bolt, 1, 1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom, 0.45, 1.4, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, base, 1.6, 0.45)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, start, finish, color, width, jitter, segments, coreColor)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local offset = finish - start
	local direction, points = offset.Unit, {start}
	segments = segments or 12
	for index = 1, segments - 1 do
		local progress = index / segments
		local random = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5) * 2
		random -= direction * random:Dot(direction)
		table.insert(points, start + offset * progress + random * jitter * (0.35 + 0.65 * math.sin(progress * math.pi)))
	end
	table.insert(points, finish)
	local parts = {}
	for index = 1, #points - 1 do
		table.insert(parts, GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, points[index], points[index + 1], width, coreColor or environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)))
		table.insert(parts, GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, points[index], points[index + 1], width * 3.2, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.72))
	end
	return parts, points
end

function GameEffects.Kill.lightningBlade(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 210, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local center = effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, center, math.random(20, 40), 3.8, 1.1, color, 0.9, 0.07, 0.15)
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local frame = camera and camera.CFrame or environment.CFrame.new()
	local bolts = {}
	local function clear()
		for _, part in bolts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		table.clear(bolts)
	end
	local function refresh()
		clear()
		for _ = 1, 4 do
			local start = center + frame.RightVector * (math.random() - 0.5) * 5 + frame.UpVector * (math.random() - 0.5) * 4
			local direction = (frame.RightVector * (math.random() - 0.5) + frame.UpVector * (math.random() - 0.5)).Unit * 2.5
			local parts = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, start, start + direction, color, 0.08, 0.5, 5)
			for _, part in parts do table.insert(bolts, part) end
		end
	end
	refresh()
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.07, refresh)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.15, refresh)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.24, clear)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.1, function()
		if body then
			body.release(GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 40 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 15, 0), nil)
			local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, white, 0.3, 0)
			environment.task.spawn(function()
				for index = 1, 10 do
					if not highlight.Parent then return end
					highlight.FillTransparency = index % 2 == 0 and 0.15 or 0.8
					environment.task.wait(0.06 / state.speed)
				end
				if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.4, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 26), FillTransparency = 0.2, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
			end)
			local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
			local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
				Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 10),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel,
				Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 0)
			emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 50, true
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.9, function()
				if body.torso and body.torso.Parent then anchor.CFrame = body.torso.CFrame end
			end, function() emitter.Enabled = false; GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 0.6) end)
		end
		local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
		local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
		local branches = {}
		for index = 1, 5 do
			local angle = index / 5 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.5
			local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X + math.cos(angle) * 4, height, effect.root.Z + math.sin(angle) * 4)
			local parts = GameEffects.Kill.lightning(state, center, position, color, 0.12, 1, 7)
			for _, part in parts do table.insert(branches, part) end
			GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, position, color, 6, 12, state.textures.spark)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.12, function() for _, part in branches do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 10, 5)
	end)
	for index = 1, 14 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, math.random() * 0.6, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, index % 3 == 0 and GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.zap3 or GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.zap1, 0.6, 1.6 + math.random() * 1.6)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshQuick, 1, 1.3)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.zap2, 0.8, 1.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.8, 0.3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.railgun(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local fallback, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then origin = effect.killer.Position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0) end
	local target = effect.chest
	local offset = target - origin
	local direction = offset.Magnitude > 0.5 and offset.Unit or fallback
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local start = origin + direction * 1.5
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(start), environment.Vector3.new(4, 4, 4))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local charge = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.25, 0.35), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 14),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Surface,
		ShapeInOut = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Inward, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 0)
	charge.Rate, charge.Enabled = 120, true
	local orb = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	orb.Shape, orb.Anchored, orb.CanCollide, orb.CanQuery, orb.CanTouch, orb.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	orb.Material, orb.Color, orb.Size, orb.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, environment.Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1), environment.CFrame.new(start)
	orb.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, orb, 0.35, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 6, 12, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rise, 0.7, 1.6, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.35, function()
		charge.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 0.5)
		pcall(function() orb:Destroy() end)
		local finish = target + direction * 45
		local beams = {GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.3, white), GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 1.2, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.45)}
		for _, beam in beams do
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.45, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.02, 0.02, beam.Size.Z)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.5)
		end
		for index = 0, math.floor((finish - start).Magnitude / 3) do
			local frame = GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, start + direction * (index * 3), direction)
			local ring = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, frame, 0.6, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, ring, 0.35, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.04, 3.5, 3.5), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ring, 0.4)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, target, color, 14, 6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, target, color, 40, 50, state.textures.spark)
		if body then
			body.release(direction * 110 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 18, 0), nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8) end)
			local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, white, 0.3, 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function()
				if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.5, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
			end)
			local trail = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(target), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
			local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, trail, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 6), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
				Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 0)
			emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 60, true
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.9, function()
				if body.torso and body.torso.Parent then trail.CFrame = body.torso.CFrame end
			end, function() emitter.Enabled = false; GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, trail, 0.6) end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sniper, 1, 0.7)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic, 0.6, 1.6, nil, 1.4)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.laser3, 0.6, 1.4)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 1.3, 0.35)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.starFall(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local gold, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 120), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.6, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	for index = 1, 7 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.1, function()
			local target = effect.chest
			if index < 7 then
				local offset = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() * 7 - 3.5, 0, math.random() * 7 - 3.5)
				target = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root + offset, effect.ignore) or effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
			local start = target + environment.Vector3.new(math.random() * 8 - 4, 26, 0) - direction * 8
			local star = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			star.Shape, star.Anchored, star.CanCollide, star.CanQuery, star.CanTouch, star.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
			star.Material, star.Color, star.Size, star.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, gold, environment.Vector3.new(0.7, 0.7, 0.7), environment.CFrame.new(start)
			star.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, star, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = colors(white, gold), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 1), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5}, 0)
			emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 70, true
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.28, function(progress) star.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(start:Lerp(target, progress * progress)) end, function()
				star.Transparency, emitter.Enabled = 1, false
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, star, 0.7)
				local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, target)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colors(white, gold), Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 2, 1, 0),
					Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
					Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Brightness = 6}, 10)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(gold), Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 5),
					Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3), Brightness = 4}, 1)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.synthSpark, 0.45, 1.5 + index * 0.08)
				if index ~= 7 or not body then return end
				body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 65, 0), nil)
				pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 5) end)
				GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, gold, white, 0.4, 0)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.33, function()
					if not body.model.Parent then return end
					local positions = {}
					for _, name in {"Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "LeftHand", "RightHand", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot"} do
						local part = body.model:FindFirstChild(name)
						if part then positions[name] = part.Position end
					end
					local beams = {}
					for _, pair in {{"Head", "UpperTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LeftHand"}, {"UpperTorso", "RightHand"}, {"LowerTorso", "LeftFoot"}, {"LowerTorso", "RightFoot"}} do
						if positions[pair[1]] and positions[pair[2]] then
							table.insert(beams, GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, positions[pair[1]], positions[pair[2]], 0.07, gold, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.1))
						end
					end
					for _, position in positions do
						local _, sparkle = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
						GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, sparkle, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colors(white, gold),
							Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.2, 1.6, 0.8, 1.4, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(60), Brightness = 6}, 1)
					end
					for _, beam in beams do
						GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 1.3, {Transparency = 1})
						GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 1.4)
					end
					pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end)
					GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.deskBell, 0.6, 1.3)
				end)
			end)
		end)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local look = camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector or environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	local direction = environment.Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
	direction = direction.Magnitude > 0.05 and direction.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	return direction, environment.Vector3.new(-direction.Z, 0, direction.X)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.meteor(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local start = position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 70, 0) + GameEffects.Kill.cameraDirection(state) * 40
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local model = environment.Instance.new("Model")
	model.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local rock = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	rock.Shape, rock.Anchored, rock.CanCollide, rock.CanQuery, rock.CanTouch = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false
	rock.Material, rock.Color, rock.Size, rock.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Slate, environment.Color3.fromRGB(45, 32, 28), environment.Vector3.new(3.2, 3.2, 3.2), environment.CFrame.new(start)
	rock.Parent = model
	local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 90), 0.55, 0)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local flame = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, rock, {Texture = state.textures.flame, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 10)),
		Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 0.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, Brightness = 3}, 0)
	flame.Rate, flame.Enabled = 110, true
	local smoke = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, rock, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 60, 55), environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 28, 26)),
		Size = sequence(0, 2.5, 1, 6), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.2),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.5), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 0)
	smoke.Rate, smoke.Enabled = 45, true
	local impact = position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.55, function(progress) rock.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(start:Lerp(impact, progress ^ 1.6)) end, function()
		flame.Enabled, smoke.Enabled = false, false
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 1.5, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 10), FillTransparency = 0.2, OutlineTransparency = 0.6})
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.2, function() if rock.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, rock, 0.5, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, model, 2.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, impact, color, 20, 6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, impact, color, 40, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, position, environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 105, 95), 18, 12, 3)
		GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 30, 0.7, state.textures.ringEdge, effect.ignore)
		GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, impact, 20, environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 60, 55), environment.Enum.Material.Slate, 0.5, 30, 1.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 11, environment.Color3.fromRGB(22, 16, 14), 3, effect.ignore, 0.15, state.textures.burn, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 9, color, 1.6, effect.ignore, 0.2, state.textures.cracks, 1)
		if body then
			local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(effect.chest.X - position.X, 0, effect.chest.Z - position.Z)
			local direction = horizontal.Magnitude > 0.1 and horizontal.Unit or GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
			body.release(direction * 20 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 55, 0), nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10) end)
			local bodyHighlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), 0.4, 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.5, function()
				if bodyHighlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, bodyHighlight, 0.6, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 8, 4), FillTransparency = 0.1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 1, 0.8, rock)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeDebris, 1, 0.7, rock)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireball2, 0.8, 0.6, rock)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, impact, 2.2, 0.6)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whoosh, 0.8, 0.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.timeStop(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local gold, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 120), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.6, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local original, gray = {}, {}
	if body then
		for _, part in body.parts do original[part] = part.Transparency end
		gray = GameEffects.Kill.transmute(state, body.parts, environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150), 0.15)
	end
	local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(value) return environment.ColorSequence.new(value, value) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.dial, Color = colors(gold), Size = sequence(0, 11, 1, 12),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 1, 0.1, 0.2, 0.85, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-90), Brightness = 3, ZOffset = -1}, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.reticle, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 6, 1, 6.5),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 1, 0.1, 0.4, 0.85, 0.5, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(140), Brightness = 3, ZOffset = -1}, 1)
	for index = 0, 3 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.28, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.click, 0.9, index % 2 == 0 and 0.7 or 0.55) end)
	end
	local target, start = effect.head, effect.head - direction * 3
	local bullet = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	bullet.Anchored, bullet.CanCollide, bullet.CanQuery, bullet.CanTouch, bullet.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
	bullet.Size, bullet.Material, bullet.Color = environment.Vector3.new(0.14, 0.14, 0.45), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, gold
	bullet.CFrame, bullet.Parent = environment.CFrame.lookAt(start, target), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, start, direction), 0.9, gold, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.15, function()
		for _, part in gray do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for part, transparency in original do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			part.Transparency = transparency
			for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then child.Transparency = 0 end end
		end
		pcall(function() ring:Destroy() end)
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.05, function(progress)
			bullet.CFrame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(start:Lerp(target, progress), target + direction)
		end, function()
			pcall(function() bullet:Destroy() end)
			if body then
				body.release(direction * 50 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 18, 0), function(result)
					if result.head then result.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * 70 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 20, 0) end
				end)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, target, gold, 10, 5)
			local _, burst = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.shards, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, gold),
				Size = sequence(0, 1.2, 1, 0.2), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(12, 24),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5}, 30)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.glassSmash, 1, 1)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sniper, 0.9, 1)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 1, 0.3)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.scratch2, 0.8, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.synthPulse, 0.5, 0.3, nil, 1.2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.supernova(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local center, white = effect.chest, environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if model then GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, white, white, 0.2, 0) end
	local frames = {}
	for index, part in body do frames[index] = {frame = part.CFrame, size = part.Size} end
	local core = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	core.Shape, core.Anchored, core.CanCollide, core.CanQuery, core.CanTouch, core.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	core.Material, core.Color, core.Size, core.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 230), environment.Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1), environment.CFrame.new(center)
	core.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local warm = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 200)
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.corona, Color = colors(warm, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 90)),
		Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5),
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(60), Brightness = 4}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 10, true
	local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color, light.Range, light.Brightness, light.Shadows, light.Parent = warm, 20, 1, false, anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.6, function(progress)
		local eased = progress * progress
		for index, part in body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local original = frames[index]
			part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center + (original.frame.Position - center) * (1 - eased)) * original.frame.Rotation
			part.Size = original.size * (1 - 0.9 * eased)
		end
		local size = 0.1 + 2.4 * progress
		core.Size, light.Brightness = environment.Vector3.new(size, size, size), 1 + 8 * progress
	end, function()
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		emitter.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, core, 0.1, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.12, function()
			pcall(function() core:Destroy() end)
			local palette = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 70), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 70),
				environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 110), environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 110, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 90, 255)}
			for index, color in palette do
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.03, function()
					local _, burst = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
					GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(color), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 10 + index * 2.2),
						Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6), Brightness = 5}, 1)
					GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0),
						Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(18, 36), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2, Brightness = 5}, 8)
				end)
			end
			local _, burst = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.needles, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 160)),
				Size = sequence(0, 6, 1, 16), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5}, 2)
			GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, white, 26, 8)
			GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, white, 2, 30, 0.7, nil, effect.ignore)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sonic2, 0.9, 1.2, nil, 2)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.7, 2, nil, 2)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom2, 0.6, 1.2)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 1.8, 0.45)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.synthPulse, 0.8, 1.2, nil, 1.2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, parts, frames)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local ok = pcall(function() context.workspace:BulkMoveTo(parts, frames, environment.Enum.BulkMoveMode.FireCFrameChanged) end)
	if ok then return end
	for index, part in parts do pcall(function() part.CFrame = frames[index] end) end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.glacier(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 225, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.8, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height - 0.3, effect.root.Z)
	local direction = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 3.5, math.random() - 0.5).Unit
	local crystals = {}
	local central, axis = GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, base, base + direction * 12, 0.9, 1.4, color, environment.Enum.Material.Ice)
	for _, part in central do
		part.Transparency, part.Reflectance = 0.1, 0.25
		local frame = part.CFrame
		part.CFrame = frame - axis * 12.5
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.22, {CFrame = frame}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		table.insert(crystals, part)
	end
	for index = 1, 6 do
		local angle = index / 6 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.4
		local start = base + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 2.8, 0, math.sin(angle) * 2.8)
		local finish = start + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 1.2, 3 + math.random() * 2.5, math.sin(angle) * 1.2)
		local parts, spikeDirection, length = GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, start, finish, 0.35, 0.6, color:Lerp(white, 0.3), environment.Enum.Material.Ice)
		for _, part in parts do
			part.Transparency = 0.15
			local frame = part.CFrame
			part.CFrame = frame - spikeDirection * (length + 0.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.05 + index * 0.03, function()
				if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.18, {CFrame = frame}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
			end)
			table.insert(crystals, part)
		end
	end
	if body then
		local frames = {}
		for index, part in body.parts do frames[index] = part.CFrame end
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.22, function(progress)
			local offset = direction * 7 * (1 - (1 - progress) ^ 2)
			local targets = {}
			for index, frame in frames do targets[index] = frame + offset end
			GameEffects.Kill.moveParts(state, body.parts, targets)
		end)
		local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, white, 0.55, 0.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2, function()
			if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.3, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(225, 240, 255), 10, 6, 2.5)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(base + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(5, 8, 5))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.25, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -4, 0), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 2}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 40, true
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 18, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.3, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1.2)
		for _, part in crystals do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, base + direction * 4, 30, color, environment.Enum.Material.Ice, 0.55, 18, 1.8)
		if body then body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), nil) end
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, base + direction * 5, color, 12, 4)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceShatter, 1, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceSplinter, 0.8, 0.7, nil, 1.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, base, 1, 0.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceSplinter, 1, 0.8, nil, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeDebris, 0.7, 1.1)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, base, 0.9, 0.3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.gore(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local center = effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local highlight = body and GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 20), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80), 1, 1)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		for index = 1, 4 do
			if highlight and highlight.Parent then
				highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = index % 2 == 0 and 0.15 or 0.75, 0.2
			end
			environment.task.wait(0.07 / state.speed)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boneSquish, 0.5, 0.45)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.3, function()
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), function(result) GameEffects.Kill.scatterBody(state, result, center, 24, 16, 12, 15) end)
			if highlight then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.6, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
		end
		local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
		local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
		local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.9, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.7, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(18, 40),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.2}, 110)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(160, 0, 18)),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 10), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 9), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 1.2}, 14)
		local rain = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center + environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(8, 1, 8))
		local rainColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 20)
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, rain, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(rainColor),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.25, 1, 0.12), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 0.9), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -70, 0),
			Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 70, true
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, rain, 2.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 40), 10, 4)
		local blood = environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12)
		GameEffects.Kill.splatRays(state, center, 16, blood, 18, effect.ignore, 1.2, 3.4, 3.4)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 7, blood, 3.4, effect.ignore, 0.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts, 1, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts, 1, 1.1, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fleshHit, 0.6, 0.6, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 1.3, 0.35)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.claws(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 40)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local frame = camera and camera.CFrame or environment.CFrame.new()
	local roll = math.random(35, 55) * (math.random() < 0.5 and -1 or 1)
	local angle = math.rad(roll)
	local direction = frame.RightVector * math.cos(angle) + frame.UpVector * math.sin(angle)
	local across = -frame.RightVector * math.sin(angle) + frame.UpVector * math.cos(angle)
	for index = -1, 1 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index + 1) * 0.02, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, effect.chest + across * index * 0.6, roll, 3.8, 1, color, 0.45, 0.07, 0.14, 0, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 220))
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08, function()
		local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
		horizontal = horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 and horizontal.Unit or GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
		if body then
			body.release(horizontal * 35 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 10, 0), nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8) end)
		end
		local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
		if ground then
			for index = -1, 1 do
				local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
				part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
				part.Transparency, part.Size = 1, environment.Vector3.new(0.35, 0.05, 0.2)
				local side = environment.Vector3.new(-horizontal.Z, 0, horizontal.X)
				local position = ground + side * index * 0.7 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.04, 0)
				part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.lookAt(position, position + horizontal)
				local decal = environment.Instance.new("Decal")
				decal.Texture, decal.Face, decal.Color3, decal.Parent = state.textures.splat, environment.Enum.NormalId.Top, environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10), part
				part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.35, 0.05, 5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.8, function() if decal.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, decal, 1.2, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 4.2)
			end
		end
		local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.6, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 22), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(70, 70), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.1}, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.bone1, 1, 0.8, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.8, 0.25)
	end)
	for index = 0, 2 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.03, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fleshSlash, 0.8, 1.1 + index * 0.12) end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.growl, 0.6, 0.4)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.bladestorm(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(215, 220, 230), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local center = effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local blades = {}
	for index = 1, 10 do
		local parts, offsets = GameEffects.Kill.blade(state, color, environment.Enum.Material.Metal, 1.6, 0.36)
		table.insert(blades, {parts = parts, offsets = offsets, phase = index / 10 * 6.283, radius = 2.1 + index % 3 * 0.35, height = -2.4 + index % 5 * 0.2})
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.2, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 1.2
		for _, blade in blades do
			local angle = blade.phase + elapsed * 14
			local position = center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * blade.radius, blade.height + progress * 4.5, math.sin(angle) * blade.radius)
			local direction = environment.Vector3.new(-math.sin(angle), 0.15, math.cos(angle)).Unit
			local frame = GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position, direction) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 30, 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(blade.parts, blade.offsets, frame)
		end
	end, function()
		for _, blade in blades do
			for _, part in blade.parts do
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 1})
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.3)
			end
		end
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), function(result)
				GameEffects.Kill.scatterBody(state, result, center, 10, 8, 10, 10)
			end)
		end
		local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.6, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 24), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.1}, 60)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rip, 1, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts2, 1, 0.9, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.9, 0.3)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function() GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12), 3, effect.ignore, 1.1) end)
	end)
	for index = 1, 6 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.17, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, center + environment.Vector3.new(0, -1 + index * 0.4, 0), math.random(0, 180), 2.8, 1,
				environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 70), 0.45, 0.05, 0.06, math.random(-40, 40))
			GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, center, white, 6, 18, state.textures.spark)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.swordWhip, 0.55, 1.3 + index * 0.1)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 165, 158), 8, 6, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.phantomBlades(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 230, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local center = effect.chest
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.2, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local blades = {}
	for index = 1, 8 do
		local angle = (index - 1) / 8 * 6.283
		local position = center + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 5, index % 2 == 0 and 0.9 or -0.6, math.sin(angle) * 5)
		local direction = (center - position).Unit
		local parts, offsets = GameEffects.Kill.blade(state, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 2.2, 0.5, 1)
		for _, part in parts do
			part.Color, part.Material = color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.25, {Transparency = 0.15})
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(parts, offsets, GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position, direction))
		table.insert(blades, {parts = parts, offsets = offsets, position = position, direction = direction})
	end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(10, 3, 10))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.5),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 20)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.55, function(progress)
		for index, blade in blades do
			local bob = math.sin(progress * 12 + index) * 0.15
			local position = blade.position + environment.Vector3.new(0, bob, 0) - blade.direction * progress * 0.8
			GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(blade.parts, blade.offsets, GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, position, blade.direction))
		end
	end, function()
		for _, blade in blades do
			local start, finish = blade.position - blade.direction * 0.8, center - blade.direction * 1.8
			GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.09, function(progress)
				GameEffects.Kill.positionBlade(blade.parts, blade.offsets, GameEffects.Kill.bladeFrame(state, start:Lerp(finish, progress * progress), blade.direction))
			end, function()
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08, function()
					for _, part in blade.parts do
						GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.2, {Transparency = 1})
						GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.25)
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.1, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, center, color, 12, 5)
			local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, center)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.shards, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
				Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 1.2, 1, 0.2), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7),
				Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(14, 26), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 4, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5}, 30)
			if body then
				body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 42, 0), nil)
				pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 9) end)
				local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, white, 0.3, 0)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.5, function()
					if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.5, {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
				end)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 14, 0.5, nil, effect.ignore)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.glassSmash, 0.9, 1.2)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.stab2, 0.8, 0.9)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 0.9, 0.3)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicZoom, 0.9, 1.6)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.magicGlow, 0.7, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rise, 0.3, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.firework(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local palette = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 90), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60), environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 255),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 90, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 255, 120)}
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 5 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 105, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 6), 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(0.8, 0.8, 0.8))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsparks, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 80)),
