		Size = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.25, 0.45), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 9),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 110, true
	local fuse = GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fuse, 0.7, 1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocket, 0.45, 1.5, anchor)
	local exploded, position = false, effect.chest
	local function explode(at)
		if exploded then return end
		exploded, emitter.Enabled = true, false
		if fuse then pcall(function() fuse:Stop() end) end
		if body then pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end) end
		local burst = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(at), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent = burst
		for index = 1, 3 do
			local color = palette[math.random(1, #palette)]
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.12, function()
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(white, color), Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 0.7, 0.5, 1, 0),
					Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(30 - index * 5, 38 - index * 5),
					SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2.2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -10, 0),
					Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 6}, 60)
				GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(color), Size = sequence(0, 4, 1, 10),
					Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35), Brightness = 4}, 1)
				GameEffects.Kill.light(state, burst, color, 10, 36, 0.6)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, index == 1 and GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.explode or GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom, 0.5, 1.1 + index * 0.12, burst)
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4, function()
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120)),
				Size = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 0), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 0.2, 0.8, 0.4, 0, 0.6, 0.8, 0.8, 0, 1, 1),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.2, 1.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 14), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
				Drag = 1.5, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -6, 0), Brightness = 5}, 40)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireworks, 0.8, 1, burst, 2.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, at, 0.9, 0.3)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, burst, 3.2)
	end
	local previous = environment.Vector3.new(0, 105, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.8, function(progress)
		if exploded or not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent then return end
		anchor.CFrame, position = body.torso.CFrame, body.torso.Position
		local velocity = body.torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		if progress > 0.08 and previous.Y > 30 and velocity.Y < previous.Y * 0.3 then explode(position) end
		previous = velocity
	end, function() explode(position) end)
	local launch = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, launch, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)),
		Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 3.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(5, 10), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(80, 80), Drag = 4, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 12)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom, 0.35, 1.8, launch)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, launch, 1.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.earthquake(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local direction, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local offset = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X - origin.X, 0, effect.root.Z - origin.Z)
	local distance = math.min(offset.Magnitude, 26)
	local path = offset.Magnitude > 0.5 and offset.Unit or direction
	local start = effect.root - path * distance
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.8, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local count = math.max(4, math.floor(distance / 2.2))
	for index = 1, count do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.045, function()
			local position = start + path * (distance * index / count)
			local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), effect.ignore)
			if not ground then return end
			local last = index == count
			local height = last and 7 or 2 + math.random() * 2.5
			local width = last and 2.6 or 1.1 + math.random() * 0.8
			local rock = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			rock.Anchored, rock.CanCollide, rock.CanQuery, rock.CanTouch, rock.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, true
			rock.Size, rock.Material = environment.Vector3.new(width, height, width), environment.Enum.Material.Slate
			rock.Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(105, 98, 92):Lerp(environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 64, 60), math.random())
			local rotation = environment.CFrame.Angles((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3, math.random() * 6.28, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3)
			rock.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(ground - environment.Vector3.new(0, height / 2 + 0.2, 0)) * rotation
			rock.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, rock, 0.12, {CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(ground + environment.Vector3.new(0, height / 2 - 0.4, 0)) * rotation}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, ground, environment.Color3.fromRGB(140, 130, 120), 3, 5, 1.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, position, 2.4, environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 26, 24), 2.2, effect.ignore, 0.1, state.textures.cracks, 1)
			if index % 2 == 0 then GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rockCrack, 0.5, 0.9 + math.random() * 0.4) end
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.5, function()
				if rock.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, rock, 0.45, {CFrame = rock.CFrame - environment.Vector3.new(0, height, 0)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, rock, 2.1)
			if not last then return end
			if body then
				body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 70, 0) + path * 10, nil)
				pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 9) end)
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, ground, environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 140, 130), 14, 10, 2.5)
			GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, ground + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 14, environment.Color3.fromRGB(95, 88, 82), environment.Enum.Material.Slate, 0.45, 26, 1.8)
			GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, ground, environment.Color3.fromRGB(210, 200, 190), 2, 20, 0.5, nil, effect.ignore)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quake, 0.9, 0.7)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rockCrack, 1, 0.7)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, ground, 1.8, 0.4)
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.root, 1, 0.9)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.sand(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, shade = environment.Color3.fromRGB(218, 188, 128), environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 120, 70)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local sand = GameEffects.Kill.transmute(state, body, environment.Enum.Material.Sand, function() return color:Lerp(shade, math.random() * 0.3) end, 0.35, false)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2, 4.4, 1.2))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 140, 90)),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.14, 1, 0.08), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -45, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1.2}, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4, function()
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 110, true
		local top = effect.head.Y + 0.8
		for _, part in sand do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local delay = (1 - math.clamp((part.Position.Y - height) / math.max(top - height, 1), 0, 1)) * 0.45
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, delay, function()
				if not part.Parent then return end
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.6, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(part.Size.X * 1.15, 0.05, part.Size.Z * 1.15),
					CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(part.Position.X, height + 0.1, part.Position.Z) * part.CFrame.Rotation}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function() if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.3, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 1)
			end)
		end
		local pile = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		pile.Shape, pile.Anchored, pile.CanCollide, pile.CanQuery, pile.CanTouch, pile.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
		pile.Material, pile.Color, pile.Size = environment.Enum.Material.Sand, color, environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.05, 0.2)
		pile.CFrame, pile.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, pile, 1.1, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(4.2, 1.7, 4.2)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.6, function()
			if pile.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, pile, 0.9, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(4.8, 0.05, 4.8), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, pile, 3.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sandPour, 0.7, 0.35, anchor, 2)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.poof, 0.6, 0.55, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.splatRays(state, position, count, color, distance, ignore, minimum, maximum, lifetime)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	local excluded = {context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)}
	for _, object in ignore or {} do if environment.typeof(object) == "Instance" then table.insert(excluded, object) end end
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = excluded
	for index = 1, count do
		local direction = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() * 1.2 - 0.8, math.random() - 0.5).Unit
		local result = context.workspace:Raycast(position, direction * (distance or 14), params)
		if not result or result.Instance.Transparency >= 0.9 then continue end
		local model = result.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then continue end
		local selected = type(color) == "function" and color(index) or color
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, result.Distance / 45, function()
			local size = (minimum or 1.2) + math.random() * ((maximum or 3) - (minimum or 1.2))
			GameEffects.Kill.groundDecal(state, result.Position, result.Normal, size, selected, lifetime or 3, 0.12, state.textures.splat, 2)
		end)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.bloodRitual(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local red, blood = environment.Color3.fromRGB(230, 20, 40), environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 12)
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local highlight = model and GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, red, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120), 0.7, 0.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 10, red, 1.6, effect.ignore, 0.5, state.textures.mandala, 1)
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, red, 9, 9, 1.5, state.textures.dial, effect.ignore)
	ring.RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(90)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore) or effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(ground + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(7, 0.2, 7))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(red, environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(5, 5), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 4}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 60, true
	local frames = {}
	for index, part in body do frames[index] = part.CFrame end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.3, function(progress)
		local rotation = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), progress * progress * 6)
		local rise = environment.Vector3.new(0, 3 * math.min(1, progress * 1.5), 0)
		for index, part in body do
			if part.Parent then part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest + rise + rotation * (frames[index].Position - effect.chest)) * rotation * frames[index].Rotation end
		end
		if highlight then highlight.FillTransparency = 0.7 - 0.5 * progress end
	end, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		local position = effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		local burst, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(190, 10, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(100, 0, 12)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.7, 1.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(16, 34),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1.2}, 80)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 0, 18)), Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 9),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 7),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 1.2}, 12)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, position, red, 16, 5)
		GameEffects.Kill.splatRays(state, position, 14, blood, 16, effect.ignore, 1.2, 3, 3.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6, blood, 3.2, effect.ignore, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.guts, 1, 0.8, burst)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.spurt, 0.7, 0.6, burst)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 1.2, 0.35)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, red, 5, 18, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whisper, 0.6, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.ghostWind, 0.6, 0.5, nil, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.disableCharacterCollisions(character)
	for _, part in character:GetDescendants() do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then pcall(function() part.CanCollide, part.CollisionGroup = false, "Characters" end) end
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.ninja(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(3, 5, 3))
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
		FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 180)),
		Size = sequence(0, 2.5, 1, 6.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.05, 0.7, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.9, 1.3),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 8), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.9, Brightness = 1.3}, 24)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 0.6, 1, 0),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(12, 20), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 5, Brightness = 5}, 12)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.leaf, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 80), environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 140, 50)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4, 2.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 8),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2.2, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -4, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-220, 220), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1.2}, 16)
	GameEffects.Kill.disableCharacterCollisions(effect.char)
	local log = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	log.Shape, log.Material, log.Color = environment.Enum.PartType.Cylinder, environment.Enum.Material.Wood, environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 84, 48)
	log.Size = environment.Vector3.new(3.6, 1.25, 1.25)
	log.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, math.random() * 6.28, math.pi / 2)
	log.CanQuery, log.CanTouch = false, false
	pcall(function() log.CollisionGroup = "Ragdolls" end)
	log.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local function launch()
		if not log.Parent then return end
		log.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * 3 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
		log.AssemblyAngularVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(direction.Z, 0, -direction.X) * 5
	end
	launch()
	local connection
	connection = context.runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() connection:Disconnect(); launch() end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.55, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.plop, 0.9, 0.55, log) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 2.6, function() if log.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, log, 0.5, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, log, 3.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.poof, 1, 1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshAiry, 0.5, 1.4, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.petrify(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, black = environment.Color3.fromRGB(125, 122, 116), environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local top, copies, converted = effect.head.Y + 1, {}, {}
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.4, 5, 1.6))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(value) return environment.ColorSequence.new(value, value) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(160, 156, 150)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 1, 2), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.5), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 30, true
	local function convert(part)
		converted[part] = true
		local copy = GameEffects.Kill.materialCopy(state, part, environment.Enum.Material.Slate, color:Lerp(black, math.random() * 0.18), 0, 0)
		if copy then table.insert(copies, copy) end
		part.Transparency = 1
		for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then child.Transparency = 1 end end
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.55, function(progress)
		local y = height + (top - height) * progress
		for _, part in body do
			if not converted[part] and part.Parent and part.Position.Y - part.Size.Y * 0.3 < y then convert(part) end
		end
	end, function()
		for _, part in body do if not converted[part] and part.Parent then convert(part) end end
		emitter.Enabled = false
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.cracks, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 38, 36)),
			Size = sequence(0, 1.3, 1, 1.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 0.8, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1, ZOffset = 0.6}, 10)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocks, 0.6, 0.55, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.05, function()
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.disableCharacterCollisions(effect.char)
		local falling = {}
		for _, part in copies do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			part.Size, part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch = part.Size * 0.9, false, true, false, false
			pcall(function() part.CollisionGroup = "Ragdolls" end)
			part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)
			table.insert(falling, part)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.7 + math.random() * 0.5, function()
				if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Size = part.Size * 0.2, Transparency = 1}) end
			end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 2.8)
		end
		local function launch()
			for _, part in falling do
				if not part.Parent then continue end
				local offset = part.Position - effect.chest
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z) * 2 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2 + math.random() * 4, 0)
				part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 4)
			end
		end
		launch()
		local connection
		connection = context.runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() connection:Disconnect(); launch() end)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 106, 100)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.3), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 14),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300, 300), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 26)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
			FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 165, 158)),
			Size = sequence(0, 2.5, 1, 6), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 0.7, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.5),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 2, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 12)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeDebris, 1, 0.9, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocks, 0.6, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.7, 0.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.blackHole(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local purple, orange, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 70, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 60), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local center = effect.chest + GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 3 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.3, 0)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local core = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	core.Shape, core.Anchored, core.CanCollide, core.CanQuery, core.CanTouch, core.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
	core.Material, core.Color, core.Size, core.CFrame = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), environment.Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1), environment.CFrame.new(center)
	core.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local glow = core:Clone()
	glow.Color, glow.Transparency, glow.Parent = purple, 0.55, GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, core, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(2.4, 2.4, 2.4)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.3, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(3, 3, 3)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	local frame = environment.CFrame.new(center) * environment.CFrame.Angles(math.rad(22), math.random() * 6.28, math.rad(12))
	local axis = frame.UpVector
	local ringAnchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, frame, environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = ringAnchor
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.vortex, Color = colors(orange, purple), Size = sequence(0, 6.5, 1, 6.5),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 1, 0.15, 0.15, 0.85, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.01),
		EmissionDirection = environment.Enum.NormalId.Top, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular,
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-260), Brightness = 4, ZOffset = 0.5}, 1, 0.05)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.ringEdge, Color = colors(purple), Size = sequence(0, 7.5, 1, 7.5),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 1, 0.15, 0.3, 0.85, 0.35, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.01),
		EmissionDirection = environment.Enum.NormalId.Top, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular,
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(180), Brightness = 3}, 1, 0.05)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(9, 9, 9))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = colors(orange, purple),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0.1), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 0.6), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35, 0.5),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 16), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Surface,
		ShapeInOut = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Inward, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 4}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 90, true
	local parts = {}
	for index, part in body do
		local offset = part.Position - center
		parts[index] = {frame = part.CFrame, offset = offset, size = part.Size, delay = 0.25 + math.clamp(offset.Magnitude / 6, 0, 1) * 0.35,
			duration = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.25, spin = (math.random() - 0.5) * 8}
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.45, function(progress)
		for index, part in body do
			local data = parts[index]
			local amount = math.clamp((progress * 1.45 - data.delay) / data.duration, 0, 1)
			if amount <= 0 or not part.Parent then continue end
			local eased = amount * amount
			local rotation = environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(axis, eased * 4.5)
			part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(center + rotation * (data.offset * (1 - eased))) * rotation * data.frame.Rotation
				* environment.CFrame.Angles(data.spin * eased, 0, data.spin * eased * 0.5)
			part.Size = data.size * (1 - 0.85 * eased)
			part.Transparency = eased > 0.85 and (eased - 0.85) / 0.15 or 0
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.45, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, core, 0.15, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, glow, 0.15, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05), Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, core, 0.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, glow, 0.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.15, function()
			local burst = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(center), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
			local burstAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
			burstAttachment.Parent = burst
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burstAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = colors(white, purple), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 12),
				Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 5, ZOffset = 1}, 2)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burstAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.ring, Color = colors(purple), Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 16),
				Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5), Brightness = 4}, 1)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burstAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = colors(white, purple), Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 1, 0),
				Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(18, 34), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
				Drag = 3, Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, 36)
			GameEffects.Kill.light(state, burst, purple, 10, 30, 0.6)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.8, 0.75, burst)
			GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, center, 1.4, 0.4)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, burst, 1.5)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, ringAnchor, purple, 5, 16, 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.vortexBlast, 0.7, 0.55, ringAnchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.suck, 0.6, 0.4, ringAnchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, ringAnchor, 2.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.midas(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local gold, pale, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 160), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local top = effect.head.Y + 1
	local disk = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.1, effect.root.Z), 3.6, pale, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.3)
	disk.Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.06, 3.6, 3.6)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.4, 5, 1.6))
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colors(white, pale),
		Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.5, 1.1, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35, 0.5), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, Brightness = 5, ZOffset = 1.5}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 18, true
	local converted, copies = {}, {}
	local function convert(part)
		converted[part] = true
		local clone = GameEffects.Kill.materialCopy(state, part, environment.Enum.Material.Foil, gold, 0.35, 0)
		if clone then table.insert(copies, clone) end
		part.Transparency = 1
		for _, child in part:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then child.Transparency = 1 end end
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.6, function(progress)
		local y = height + (top - height) * progress
		disk.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, y, effect.root.Z) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
		for _, part in body do
			if not converted[part] and part.Parent and part.Position.Y - part.Size.Y * 0.3 < y then convert(part) end
		end
	end, function()
		for _, part in body do if not converted[part] and part.Parent then convert(part) end end
		if disk.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, disk, 0.2, {Transparency = 1}) end
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, disk, 0.25)
		local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		attachment.Parent, attachment.WorldPosition = anchor, effect.head + environment.Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 4, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.45), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(120), Brightness = 8, ZOffset = 2}, 1)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.3, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		for _, parts in {copies, body} do for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end
		GameEffects.Kill.scatter(state, effect.chest, 34, gold, environment.Enum.Material.Foil, 6, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = colors(white, gold), Size = sequence(0, 0.7, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 20), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Drag = 4, Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 24)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(pale, gold), Size = sequence(0, 0.18, 1, 0),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
			ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, Brightness = 3}, 40)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.glass, 0.9, 0.85, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.cash, 0.5, 1.2, anchor)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.shopbell, 0.6, 1, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, gold, 5, 14, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.tornado(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), nil, 3.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local clouds = {}
	for index = 1, 7 do
		local y = (index - 1) * 2
		local radius = 0.8 + y * 0.3
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(radius, y, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(0.6, 0.6, 0.6))
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(215, 215, 222), environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 162)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.9 + y * 0.12, 1, 1.6 + y * 0.18), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.45, 1, 1),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.2), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 55, true
		table.insert(clouds, {anchor = anchor, emitter = emitter, height = y, radius = radius, phase = math.random() * 6.28})
	end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(5, 0.4, 5))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local rockColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 84, 78)
	local rocks = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.rock, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(rockColor, rockColor),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 0.25), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 14),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(20, 20), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -12, 0), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-400, 400), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 0)
	rocks.Rate, rocks.Enabled = 22, true
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.vortex, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210), 3, 9, 1.6, 1.5)
	ring.RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-300)
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.8, function(progress)
		local elapsed = progress * 1.8
		local scale = math.min(1, elapsed / 0.3) * (elapsed > 1.5 and math.max(0, 1 - (elapsed - 1.5) / 0.3) or 1)
		for _, cloud in clouds do
			local angle = cloud.phase + elapsed * 11
			cloud.anchor.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * cloud.radius * scale, cloud.height * scale, math.sin(angle) * cloud.radius * scale))
		end
		if not body or elapsed >= 1.35 then return end
		for _, part in body.body do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local offset = part.Position - position
			local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z)
			local distance = horizontal.Magnitude
			local radial = distance > 0.05 and horizontal / distance or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
			part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(-radial.Z, 0, radial.X) * 24 + radial * (2.2 - distance) * 6
				+ environment.Vector3.new(0, offset.Y < 9 and 9 or 0, 0)
		end
	end, function()
		for _, cloud in clouds do cloud.emitter.Enabled = false; GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, cloud.anchor, 1) end
		rocks.Enabled = false
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.35, function()
		if not body or not body.torso or not body.torso.Parent then return end
		local offset = body.torso.Position - position
		local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z)
		local direction = horizontal.Magnitude > 0.05 and horizontal.Unit or GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
		for _, part in body.body do pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * 55 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 22, 0) end) end
		pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8) end)
	end)
	local sound = GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.windHowl, 0.6, 0.45, anchor, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.8, function() if sound and sound.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, sound, 0.4, {Volume = 0}) end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 0.45, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.hologram(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 230, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local hologram = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color = color, transparency = 0.72})
	local field = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.ForceField, color = color, transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local parts = {}
	for _, part in hologram do table.insert(parts, part) end
	for _, part in field do table.insert(parts, part) end
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		for index = 1, 4 do
			for _, part in hologram do if part.Parent then part.Transparency = index % 2 == 0 and 0.72 or 0.9 end end
			environment.task.wait(0.05 / state.speed)
		end
	end)
	local top = effect.head.Y + 0.9
	local scanner = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	scanner.Anchored, scanner.CanCollide, scanner.CanQuery, scanner.CanTouch, scanner.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
	scanner.Material, scanner.Color, scanner.Transparency, scanner.Size = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, color, 0.35, environment.Vector3.new(4.2, 0.05, 4.2)
	scanner.CFrame, scanner.Parent = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, top, effect.root.Z), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local glow = scanner:Clone()
	glow.Transparency, glow.Size, glow.Parent = 0.82, environment.Vector3.new(5.6, 0.03, 5.6), GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.22, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1.5, 4), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), Drag = 2,
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, Brightness = 4}, 0)
	local scanned = {}
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.22, function()
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 0.85, function(progress)
			local y = top + (height - top) * progress
			scanner.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, y, effect.root.Z)
			glow.CFrame = scanner.CFrame
			for _, part in parts do
				if scanned[part] or not part.Parent or part.Position.Y <= y then continue end
				scanned[part] = true
				if part.Material == environment.Enum.Material.Neon then
					anchor.CFrame, anchor.Size = part.CFrame, part.Size
					emitter:Emit(math.clamp(math.floor(part.Size.X * part.Size.Y * part.Size.Z * 3), 3, 12))
				end
				part.Transparency = 1
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.1)
			end
		end, function()
			for _, part in {scanner, glow} do
				if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.2, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(6.5, 0.02, 6.5)}) end
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.25)
			end
			for _, part in parts do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
			local floor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.1, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(1, 0.2, 1))
			GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, floor, state.textures.ring, color, 1, 8, 0.45, 4)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, floor, 1)
		end)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 4, 12, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.cameraClicks(state, anchor, 5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sciSynth, 0.4, 0.7, anchor, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.shadow(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local dark, purple = environment.Color3.fromRGB(8, 6, 12), environment.Color3.fromRGB(140, 60, 255)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local parts = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, color = dark, transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local right = camera and camera.CFrame.RightVector or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local view = camera and (camera.CFrame.Position - effect.head).Unit or environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		local eye = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		eye.Shape, eye.Anchored, eye.CanCollide, eye.CanQuery, eye.CanTouch, eye.CastShadow = environment.Enum.PartType.Ball, true, false, false, false, false
		eye.Material, eye.Color, eye.Size = environment.Enum.Material.Neon, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 50), environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)
		eye.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(effect.head + view * 0.62 + right * 0.2 * side + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0))
		eye.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, eye, 0.15, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.26, 0.26, 0.26)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.45, function()
			if eye.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, eye, 0.2, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.02, 0.02, 0.02), Transparency = 1}) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, eye, 0.8)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4, function()
		for _, part in parts do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local duration = 0.5 + math.clamp((part.Position.Y - height) / 6, 0, 1) * 0.5
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, duration, {CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(part.Position.X, height + 0.1, part.Position.Z) * part.CFrame.Rotation,
				Size = environment.Vector3.new(part.Size.X * 1.5, 0.1, part.Size.Z * 1.5)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, duration, function() if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.8, {Transparency = 1}) end end)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, duration + 0.9)
		end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.45, function() GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 5.5, dark, 1.4, effect.ignore, 0.8) end)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.2, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(3.5, 0.2, 3.5))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.ribbon, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 20, 90), environment.Color3.fromRGB(10, 5, 20)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 0.4, 1.4, 1, 0.3), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 8), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(10, 10), Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel,
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 1.5}, 18, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
		FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(14, 10, 20)), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 4),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0.25, 0.7, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.9, 1.3), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 2.5),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(50, 50), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 10, 0.4)
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.5, function()
		if attachment.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.ringEdge, purple, 1, 7, 0.8, 2) end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, purple, 3, 12, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.slime, 0.8, 0.7, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.ghostWind, 0.6, 0.8, anchor, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.portalDisk(state, position, normal, color)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local reference = math.abs(normal.Z) < 0.9 and environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local frame = environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(position, normal:Cross(reference).Unit, normal)
	local outer = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, frame, 0.2, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0)
	local inner = GameEffects.Kill.disc(state, frame + normal * 0.03, 0.15, environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, outer, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 4.6, 4.6)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, inner, 0.25, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.06, 3.9, 3.9)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, frame + normal * 0.08, environment.Vector3.new(1, 0.1, 1))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, state.textures.vortex, color, 3.8, 3.8, 1.3, 3)
	ring.RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-320)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.spark, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.4, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 7), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Brightness = 4}, 14)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 12, 1.2)
	return function()
		for _, part in {outer, inner} do
			if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.2, {Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.25)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 0.3)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.portal(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local orange, blue = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 30), environment.Color3.fromRGB(40, 160, 255)
	local ground, normal = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	ground, normal = ground or effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), normal or environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	local closeGround = GameEffects.Kill.portalDisk(state, ground + normal * 0.03, normal, orange)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, -6, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 2), 3.4, function(result)
		for _, part in result.parts do part.CanCollide = false end
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local exitGround = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root + direction * 6, effect.ignore) or ground + direction * 6
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)}
	local hit = context.workspace:Raycast(exitGround + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), environment.Vector3.new(0, 14, 0), params)
	local height = hit and math.max(4.5, hit.Distance - 1) or 11
	local exit = exitGround + environment.Vector3.new(0, math.min(height, 11), 0)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.35, function()
		local closeExit = GameEffects.Kill.portalDisk(state, exit, environment.Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), blue)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function()
			if body and body.model.Parent and body.torso and body.torso.Parent then
				local offset = exit - environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.4, 0) - body.torso.Position
				for _, part in body.body do
					pcall(function()
						part.CFrame += offset
						part.CanCollide = true
						part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = environment.Vector3.new(0, -22, 0) + direction * 3
					end)
				end
			end
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.teleport, 0.8, 1.3)
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2, closeExit)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, closeGround)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.teleport, 1, 0.9)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshAiry, 0.6, 0.7)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.blastOff(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local side = environment.Vector3.new(-direction.Z, 0, direction.X) * (math.random() < 0.5 and -1 or 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, direction * 26 + side * 6 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 95, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 10), 2.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.upForce(state, body, 0.55)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local groundAnchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.3, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(2, 0.4, 2))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = groundAnchor
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core,
		Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 200), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 60)),
		Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 6), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3),
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 4}, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, groundAnchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft,
		Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 250), environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 205)),
		Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 4), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 0.9),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(8, 14), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85), Drag = 5,
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 16)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(1.5, 1.5, 1.5))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft,
		Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245), environment.Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)),
		Size = sequence(0, 1, 1, 2.8), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.5), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.2, LightInfluence = 0.6, Brightness = 1.2}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 70, true
	local position = effect.chest
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.9, function()
		if body and body.torso and body.torso.Parent then
			position = body.torso.Position
			anchor.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position)
		end
	end, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		if body then pcall(function() body.model:Destroy() end) end
		local burst = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))
		local burstAttachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
		burstAttachment.Parent = burst
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burstAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 170)),
			Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 9, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.55), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(160), Brightness = 8, ZOffset = 2}, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burstAttachment, {Texture = state.textures.sparkBright, Color = colors(white, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 150)),
			Size = sequence(0, 1.2, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Brightness = 6}, 8)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, burst, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colors(white), Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 5),
			Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.7), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.2}, 8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.timerBell, 0.8, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, burst, 1.2)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1.2)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.rocket, 0.6, 1.1, groundAnchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boom, 0.4, 1.7, groundAnchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.root, 0.6, 0.25)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, groundAnchor, 2)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.ricochet(state, effect)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local direction, origin = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	if effect.killer and effect.killer.Parent then origin = effect.killer.Position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0) end
	local target = effect.head
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.RespectCanCollide = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {effect.char, context.workspace.CurrentCamera, GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state), context.player.Character}
	local points = {origin}
	local aim, last = target - origin, origin
	aim = aim.Magnitude > 0.5 and aim.Unit or direction
	for index = 1, 3 do
		local shot = (aim + environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6, math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6).Unit
		local hit = context.workspace:Raycast(last, shot * 28, params)
		last = hit and hit.Position or effect.chest + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(index * 2.1) * 6, math.random() * 3, math.sin(index * 2.1) * 6)
		table.insert(points, last)
	end
	table.insert(points, target)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.4, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	for index = 1, #points - 1 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (index - 1) * 0.08, function()
			local start, finish = points[index], points[index + 1]
			local beams = {GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.08, white),
				GameEffects.Kill.beam(state, start, finish, 0.3, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.5)}
			for _, beam in beams do
				GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, beam, 0.5, {Transparency = 1, Size = environment.Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, beam.Size.Z)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, beam, 0.55)
			end
			if index > 1 then
				GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, start, color, 10, 18, state.textures.spark)
				GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.ding, 0.5, 2 + math.random() * 0.4)
			end
		end)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, (#points - 1) * 0.08, function()
		local impact = points[#points] - points[#points - 1]
		impact = impact.Magnitude > 0.1 and impact.Unit or direction
		if body then
			body.release(impact * 45 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 12, 0), function(result)
				if result.head then result.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = impact * 65 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 15, 0) end
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, target, color, 8, 5)
		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, target)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.splat,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 18), environment.Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 10)),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0.2), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(10, 18), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -60, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), LightEmission = 0, Brightness = 1}, 24)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.snap, 0.9, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, target, 0.8, 0.25)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sniper, 0.9, 1.1)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.volcano(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 20)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local position = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X, height, effect.root.Z)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), nil, 3.6, nil, true)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 8, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30), 2.2, effect.ignore, 0.3, state.textures.cracks, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, position, environment.Color3.fromRGB(110, 95, 85), 6, 4, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 0.6, 0.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.quakeRumble, 0.7, 0.6, nil, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.4, function()
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(2, 0.4, 2))
		local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
		local flame = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.flame,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new({environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 140)),
				environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, color), environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, environment.Color3.fromRGB(120, 20, 5))}),
			Size = sequence(0, 1.3, 1, 0.5), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 0.8), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.2),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(40, 60), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(8, 8), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -80, 0),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 0)
		flame.Rate, flame.Enabled = 130, true
		local smoke = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 55, 50), environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 26, 24)),
			Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 8), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.3, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4, 2),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(14, 22), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(12, 12), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
			LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 0)
		smoke.Rate, smoke.Enabled = 30, true
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.1, function() flame.Enabled, smoke.Enabled = false, false end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 12, 26, 1.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 14, color, environment.Enum.Material.Neon, 0.5, 35, 1.8)
		if body then
			body.release(environment.Vector3.new(0, 75, 0) + GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect) * 6, nil)
			pcall(function() body.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8) end)
			local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), 0.35, 0)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.6, function()
				if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.6, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 8, 4), FillTransparency = 0.1, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
			end)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 7, environment.Color3.fromRGB(22, 16, 14), 3, effect.ignore, 0.2, state.textures.burn, 2)
		GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 4, color, 2.2, effect.ignore, 0.3, state.textures.splat, 1)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), color, 12, 5)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireball2, 0.9, 0.7, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireWhoosh, 0.7, 0.5, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.boomDeep, 0.7, 0.6, anchor)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, position, 1.6, 0.5)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, position, color, startSize, endSize, duration, texture, ignore)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, position, ignore) or position
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(ground + environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(0.4, 0.2, 0.4))
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	local ring = GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, attachment, texture or state.textures.ringEdge, color, startSize, endSize, duration, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, duration + 0.5)
	return ring, anchor
end

function GameEffects.Kill.hellfire(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 20)
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, direction * 2 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 2), 3.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 9, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30), 1.6, effect.ignore, 0.25, state.textures.cracks, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.splat(state, effect.root, 6, environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 10, 8), 2.4, effect.ignore, 0.5, state.textures.burn, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.ringAtGround(state, effect.root, color, 2, 12, 0.8, state.textures.ringEdge, effect.ignore)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore) or effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	for index = 0, 5 do
		local angle, radius = index / 6 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.4, index == 0 and 0 or 3
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(ground + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0.2, math.sin(angle) * radius)),
			environment.Vector3.new(1.2, 0.2, 1.2))
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.flame,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new({environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 170)),
				environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, color), environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 20, 5))}),
			Size = sequence(0, 1.4, 0.5, 2.2, 1, 0.4), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.4, 0.15, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.8),
			Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(index == 0 and 30 or 18, index == 0 and 45 or 28), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(8, 8),
			Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(-20, 20), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 3}, 0)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.08, function()
			if emitter.Parent then emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = index == 0 and 140 or 70, true end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.2 + index * 0.05, function() if emitter.Parent then emitter.Enabled = false end end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.4)
	end
	if body then
		local highlight = GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, body.model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), 0.3, 0)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.7, function()
			if highlight.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, highlight, 0.6, {FillColor = environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 8, 4), FillTransparency = 0.05, OutlineTransparency = 1}) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.9, function() for _, part in body.parts do pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end) end end)
	end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(ground + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(4, 1, 4))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsparks, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), color),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.4, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 16),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(30, 30), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5}, 50, 0.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsmoke, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(60, 40, 35), environment.Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 15)),
		Size = sequence(0, 3, 1, 8), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.4, 2), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(4, 8),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(25, 25), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 14, 0.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 10, 24, 1.8)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.sear, 0.8, 0.45, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireball, 0.7, 0.6, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.demon, 0.5, 0.8, anchor, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.root, 1, 0.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.materialCopy(state, part, material, color, reflectance, transparency, keepDecals)
	local ok, clone = pcall(function() return part:Clone() end)
	if not ok or not clone then return nil end
	if type(color) == "function" then color = color() end
	for _, child in clone:GetChildren() do
		if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") then child.TextureId = ""; continue end
		if keepDecals and child:IsA("Decal") then continue end
		child:Destroy()
	end
	pcall(function() if clone:IsA("MeshPart") then clone.TextureID = "" end end)
	clone.Material, clone.Color, clone.Transparency = material, color, transparency or 0
	if reflectance ~= false then clone.Reflectance = reflectance or 0 end
	clone.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
	return clone
end

function GameEffects.Kill.transmute(state, parts, material, color, duration, reflectance, keepDecals)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local result = {}
	for _, part in parts do
		local clone = GameEffects.Kill.materialCopy(state, part, material, color, reflectance, 1, keepDecals)
		if clone then
			clone.Anchored, clone.CanCollide, clone.CanQuery, clone.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
			table.insert(result, clone)
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, clone, duration, {Transparency = 0}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, duration, {Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		for _, child in part:GetChildren() do
			if child:IsA("Decal") then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, child, duration, {Transparency = 1}) end
		end
	end
	return result
end

function GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, position, color, count, speed, size)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(position), environment.Vector3.new(2, 0.5, 2))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.smokeFB, FlipbookLayout = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4,
		FlipbookMode = environment.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, (size or 2) * 0.6, 1, (size or 2) * 1.6),
		Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.2, 0.7, 0.45, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(speed * 0.4, speed), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85), Drag = 3,
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, count)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.5)
	return anchor
end

function GameEffects.Kill.frostBite(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(160, 225, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, effect.chest, 30, 3.4, 1.05, color, 0.8, 0.07, 0.08, -10)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.08, function() GameEffects.Kill.arc(state, effect.chest, 150, 3.4, 1.05, color, 0.8, 0.07, 0.08, 10) end)
	local ice = GameEffects.Kill.transmute(state, body, environment.Enum.Material.Ice, color, 0.25, 0.25)
	local spikes = {}
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.2, function()
		for _ = 1, 10 do
			local part = ice[math.random(1, math.max(1, #ice))]
			if not part then continue end
			local direction = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() * 0.8, math.random() - 0.5).Unit
			local pieces = GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, part.Position, part.Position + direction * (0.9 + math.random() * 0.8), 0.14, 0.2,
				white:Lerp(color, 0.5), environment.Enum.Material.Glass)
			for _, piece in pieces do piece.Transparency = 0.2; table.insert(spikes, piece) end
		end
	end)
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(2.6, 5, 2))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.25, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.3),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.5), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -3, 0),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 2}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 40, true
	GameEffects.Kill.smoke(state, effect.root - environment.Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), environment.Color3.fromRGB(220, 235, 255), 8, 3, 2.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.95, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		for _, group in {ice, body, spikes} do for _, part in group do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end end
		GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, effect.chest, 28, color, environment.Enum.Material.Ice, 0.4, 16, 1.8)
		local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, effect.chest)
		GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, white),
			Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 1.2, 1, 3.5), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.3, 1, 1),
			Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180),
			Drag = 3, LightEmission = 0.3, Brightness = 2}, 20)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, effect.chest, color, 10, 4)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceShatter, 1, 1.1)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceCrack, 0.7, 1.3, nil, 1.5)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.7, 0.25)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 4, 14, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 2.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.whooshQuick, 0.9, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceCrack, 1, 1, nil, 1.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.rainbow(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local colors = {environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 70), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 60), environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 100),
		environment.Color3.fromRGB(70, 150, 255), environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 255)}
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colorSequence = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local direction = GameEffects.Kill.direction(state, effect)
	local body = GameEffects.Kill.body(state, effect, direction * 26 + environment.Vector3.new(0, 60, 0), GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 4), 3.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if body and body.torso then
		local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(body.torso.Position), environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2))
		local trails = {}
		for index, color in colors do
