			local first, second = environment.Instance.new("Attachment"), environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
			first.Position, first.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0, (3.5 - index) * 0.36 + 0.18, 0), anchor
			second.Position, second.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(0, (3.5 - index) * 0.36 - 0.18, 0), anchor
			local trail = environment.Instance.new("Trail")
			trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1 = first, second
			trail.Color, trail.Transparency = colorSequence(color), sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1)
			trail.Lifetime, trail.LightEmission, trail.FaceCamera, trail.Parent = 1.1, 0.6, true, anchor
			table.insert(trails, trail)
		end
		local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.star, Color = colorSequence(white, colors[3]),
			Size = sequence(0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 3),
			SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Brightness = 4}, 0)
		emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 30, true
		GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.7, function()
			if body.torso.Parent then anchor.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(body.torso.Position) end
		end, function()
			emitter.Enabled = false
			for _, trail in trails do trail.Enabled = false end
			local _, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, anchor.Position)
			GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = colorSequence(white, colors[3]),
				Size = sequence(0, 0, 0.3, 2, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12),
				SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3, Brightness = 6}, 14)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.6, 1.5)
		end)
	end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root), environment.Vector3.new(2, 1, 2))
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.soft, Color = colorSequence(white), Size = sequence(0, 1.5, 1, 4),
		Transparency = sequence(0, 0.1, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.6, 1), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(6, 12),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(85, 85), Drag = 4, Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 0.8, Brightness = 1.3}, 16)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 1.5)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.slideWhistle, 0.6, 1.2)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.partyHorn2, 0.6, 1.1)
	for index = 0, 2 do
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 0.25 + index * 0.09, function() GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.chimes, 0.4, 1.2 + index * 0.25) end)
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.inferno(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local parts = GameEffects.Kill.ghost(state, effect.char, {material = environment.Enum.Material.Neon,
		color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 60), transparency = 0})
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	for _, part in parts do
		GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.9, {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(28, 14, 8)}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	end
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1, function()
		for _, part in parts do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local offset = math.max(0, part.Position.Y - (height + 0.3)) * 0.85
			GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.55, {CFrame = part.CFrame - environment.Vector3.new(0, offset, 0), Size = part.Size * 0.55,
				Transparency = 1}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, environment.Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, 0.6)
		end
	end)

	local sequence = function(...) return GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, ...) end
	local colors = function(first, second) return environment.ColorSequence.new(first, second or first) end
	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest - environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)), environment.Vector3.new(2.4, 4.4, 1.6))
	local flame = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.flame,
		Color = environment.ColorSequence.new({environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 170)),
			environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 30)),
			environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 30, 10))}),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.8, 0.3, 1.8, 1, 0.2), Transparency = sequence(0, 0.6, 0.15, 0.05, 0.8, 0.4, 1, 1),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.45, 0.8), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 5), Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 9, 0),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(15, 15), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(-15, 15), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		ShapeStyle = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, Brightness = 3, ZOffset = 1}, 30)
	flame.Rate, flame.Enabled = 110, true
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.25, function() if flame.Parent then flame.Enabled = false end end)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.fsparks, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120), color),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.35, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.4), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(2, 6),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(50, 50), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
		Brightness = 4}, 30, 0.3)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.square, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(80, 75, 70), environment.Color3.fromRGB(30, 28, 26)),
		Size = sequence(0, 0.2, 1, 0.08), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 1.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.5, 2),
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -4, 0), Drag = 1, Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-200, 200),
		Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Brightness = 1}, 50, 1)
	local attachment = environment.Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = colors(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 160), color),
		Size = sequence(0, 2, 1, 7), Transparency = sequence(0, 0, 1, 1), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35),
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = 4}, 2)
	local floor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.root.X, height + 0.1, effect.root.Z), environment.Vector3.new(1, 0.2, 1))
	GameEffects.Kill.groundRing(state, floor, state.textures.ringEdge, color, 2, 11, 0.55, 3)
	local light = environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color, light.Range, light.Shadows, light.Brightness, light.Parent = color, 16, false, 0, anchor
	GameEffects.Kill.animate(state, 1.7, function(progress) light.Brightness = (1 - progress) * (4 + math.noise(progress * 22, 0.37) * 4) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireball, 0.45, 0.55, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.fireWhoosh, 0.6, 0.5, anchor)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, floor, 1.5)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, outline, transparency, outlineTransparency)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local highlight = environment.Instance.new("Highlight")
	highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor = color, outline or color
	highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = transparency or 0.5, outlineTransparency or 0
	highlight.DepthMode = environment.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
	highlight.Adornee, highlight.Parent = model, model
	return highlight
end

function GameEffects.Kill.crystalPrison(state, effect)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local color, white = environment.Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 255), environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local ground = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, effect.root, effect.ignore)
	local height = ground and ground.Y or effect.root.Y - 3
	local body, model = GameEffects.Kill.staticBody(state, effect)
	GameEffects.Kill.hideEffectBody(state, effect.char)
	if model then GameEffects.Kill.highlight(state, model, color, environment.Color3.fromRGB(230, 200, 255), 0.45, 0) end

	local crystals = {}
	local top = effect.head + environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
	for index = 1, 9 do
		local angle, radius = index / 9 * 6.283 + math.random() * 0.3, 2.2 + math.random() * 0.6
		local start = environment.Vector3.new(effect.root.X + math.cos(angle) * radius, height - 0.2, effect.root.Z + math.sin(angle) * radius)
		local finish = top + environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 0.4, math.random() * 0.8, math.sin(angle) * 0.4)
		local parts, direction, length = GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, start, finish, 0.45, 0.6 + math.random() * 0.25,
			color:Lerp(white, math.random() * 0.3), environment.Enum.Material.Glass)
		for _, part in parts do
			part.Transparency, part.Reflectance = 0.2, 0.3
			local frame = part.CFrame
			part.CFrame = frame - direction * (length + 0.3)
			GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, index * 0.035, function()
				if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.3, {CFrame = frame}, environment.Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
			end)
			table.insert(crystals, part)
		end
	end

	local anchor = GameEffects.Kill.anchor(state, environment.CFrame.new(effect.chest), environment.Vector3.new(4, 5, 4))
	local emitter = GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, anchor, {Texture = state.textures.star4, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0, 0.5, 0.9, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.5),
		Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 90), Shape = environment.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Brightness = 5, ZOffset = 1}, 0)
	emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 14, true
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 5, 16, 1.4)
	GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, anchor, 3)
	GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, 1.15, function()
		emitter.Enabled = false
		for _, part in crystals do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		for _, part in body do pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
		GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, effect.chest, 26, color, environment.Enum.Material.Glass, 0.45, 18, 1.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, effect.chest, color, 12, 5)
		GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, effect.chest, color, 30, 30, state.textures.shards)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.glassSmash2, 1, 0.8)
		GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceFreeze, 0.6, 0.7, nil, 1.6)
		GameEffects.Kill.shakeCamera(state, effect.chest, 0.9, 0.3)
	end)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.iceFreeze, 0.8, 0.6, nil, 1.6)
	GameEffects.Kill.sound(state, GameEffects.Kill.soundIds.synthSpark, 0.4, 1.6)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.spike(state, start, finish, width, depth, color, material)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local offset = finish - start
	local length, direction = offset.Magnitude, offset.Unit
	local reference = math.abs(direction.Z) < 0.9 and environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local frame = environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(start, direction:Cross(reference).Unit, direction)
	local parts = {}
	for index = 0, 3 do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("WedgePart")
		part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch, part.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
		part.Material, part.Color, part.Reflectance = material, color, 0.15
		part.Size = environment.Vector3.new(width, length, depth)
		part.CFrame = frame * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, index * math.pi / 2, 0) * environment.CFrame.new(0, length / 2, -depth / 2)
		part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		table.insert(parts, part)
	end
	return parts, direction, length
end

function GameEffects.Kill.fragments(state, position, count, color, material, size, speed, lifetime)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local parts = {}
	for _ = 1, count do
		local part = environment.Instance.new("Part")
		local scale = size * (0.5 + math.random())
		part.Size = environment.Vector3.new(scale, scale * (0.6 + math.random() * 0.6), scale * (0.7 + math.random() * 0.5))
		part.Material, part.Color = material or environment.Enum.Material.Slate, color
		part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch = false, false
		pcall(function() part.CollisionGroup = "Ragdolls" end)
		local offset = environment.Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5, math.random() * 0.8, (math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5)
		part.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(position + offset) * environment.CFrame.Angles(math.random() * 6, math.random() * 6, math.random() * 6)
		part.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.bodiesFolder(state)
		table.insert(parts, part)
		local duration = (lifetime or 2) + math.random() * 0.6
		GameEffects.Kill.delay(state, duration, function()
			if part.Parent then GameEffects.Kill.tween(state, part, 0.4, {Size = part.Size * 0.2, Transparency = 1}) end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.destroyLater(state, part, duration + 0.5)
	end

	local function launch()
		for _, part in parts do
			if not part.Parent then continue end
			local direction = environment.Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0, math.random() - 0.5)
			direction = direction.Magnitude > 0.05 and direction.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
			part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * speed * (0.4 + math.random() * 0.6) + environment.Vector3.new(0, speed * (0.6 + math.random() * 0.6), 0)
			part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = GameEffects.Kill.randomVector(state, 8)
		end
	end
	launch()
	local connection
	connection = context.runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() connection:Disconnect(); launch() end)
	return parts
end

function GameEffects.Kill.flash(state, position, color, size, brightness)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = state.textures.core, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, size * 0.2, 1, size), Transparency = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0, 1, 1),
		Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.35), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), Brightness = brightness or 5, ZOffset = 1}, 2)
	GameEffects.Kill.light(state, anchor, color, 10, math.min(60, size * 3), 0.5)
	return anchor, attachment
end

function GameEffects.Kill.sparks(state, position, color, count, speed, texture)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local anchor, attachment = GameEffects.Kill.effectAttachment(state, position)
	local white = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	GameEffects.Kill.particle(state, attachment, {Texture = texture or state.textures.fsparks, Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(white, color),
		Size = GameEffects.Kill.numberSequence(state, 0, 0.5, 1, 0), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.7),
		Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(speed * 0.5, speed), SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 180), Drag = 3,
		Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, -25, 0), Orientation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, Brightness = 5}, count)
	return anchor, attachment
end

function GameEffects.Kill.track(state, signal, callback)
	local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
	table.insert(state.connections, connection)
	return connection
end

function GameEffects.Kill.observeCharacter(state, player, character)
	if state.hooked[character] then return end
	state.hooked[character] = true

	state.context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
		if not humanoid or not state.alive then return end

		local handled, connections = false, {}
		local function onDeath()
			if handled then return end
			handled = true
			GameEffects.Kill.onDeath(state, player, character)
		end

		table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(onDeath))
		table.insert(connections, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
			if health <= 0 then onDeath() end
		end))
		table.insert(connections, character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
			if parent then return end
			for _, connection in connections do connection:Disconnect() end
			state.characterConnections[character], state.hooked[character] = nil, nil
		end))
		state.characterConnections[character] = connections
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.observePlayer(state, player)
	if player == state.context.player then return end
	if player.Character then GameEffects.Kill.observeCharacter(state, player, player.Character) end
	GameEffects.Kill.track(state, player.CharacterAdded, function(character)
		GameEffects.Kill.observeCharacter(state, player, character)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.observeBlade(state, blade)
	if blade.Name ~= "ThrowingKnife" then return end
	local environment = state.context.environment

	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local part = blade:FindFirstChild("BladePosition") or blade:WaitForChild("BladePosition", 1)
		if not part and blade:IsA("BasePart") then part = blade end

		local started = environment.os.clock()
		while part and part.Parent and blade.Parent and state.alive and environment.os.clock() - started < 6 do
			GameEffects.Kill.recordBlade(state, part.Position)
			environment.task.wait(0.03)
		end
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.observe(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	for _, player in context.players:GetPlayers() do GameEffects.Kill.observePlayer(state, player) end

	GameEffects.Kill.track(state, context.players.PlayerAdded, function(player) GameEffects.Kill.observePlayer(state, player) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.track(state, context.workspace.DescendantAdded, function(model) GameEffects.Kill.recordRagdoll(state, model) end)
	GameEffects.Kill.track(state, context.workspace.ChildAdded, function(blade) GameEffects.Kill.observeBlade(state, blade) end)

	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local services = context.storage:WaitForChild("ClientServices", 30)
		local weapons = services and services:WaitForChild("WeaponService", 30)
		local fired = weapons and weapons:WaitForChild("GunFired", 30)
		if not state.alive or not fired or not fired:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return end

		GameEffects.Kill.track(state, fired.OnClientEvent, function(shooter, _, position, hit)
			GameEffects.Kill.recordShot(state, shooter, position, hit)
		end)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.stop(state)
	state.alive = false
	state.showing = false
	GameEffects.Kill.resetShake(state)

	for _, connection in state.connections do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
	for _, connections in state.characterConnections do
		for _, connection in connections do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
	end

	table.clear(state.connections)
	table.clear(state.characterConnections)
	if state.folder then pcall(function() state.folder:Destroy() end); state.folder = nil end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.preview(state, name, options, title, generation)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then return end

		if not state.dummyDescription then
			pcall(function() state.dummyDescription = context.players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(context.player.UserId) end)
		end
		if not state.dummyDescription then return end

		local ok, model = pcall(function()
			return context.players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(state.dummyDescription, environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
		end)
		if not ok or not model then return end

		model.Name = "PulseKillPreview"
		local frame = camera.CFrame
		local direction = environment.Vector3.new(frame.LookVector.X, 0, frame.LookVector.Z)
		direction = direction.Magnitude > 0.01 and direction.Unit or environment.Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		local character = context.player.Character
		local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local position = (root and root.Position or frame.Position) + direction * 9
		local localHumanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local ground
		if root then ground = root.Position.Y - root.Size.Y / 2 - (localHumanoid and localHumanoid.HipHeight or 2) end
		if not ground then
			local hit = GameEffects.Kill.ground(state, position, {a = character, b = camera})
			ground = hit and hit.Y or position.Y - 3
		end

		local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local previewRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		previewRoot.Anchored = true
		for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch = false, false, false end
		end
		if humanoid then humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = environment.Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None end

		local center = environment.Vector3.new(position.X, ground + (humanoid and humanoid.HipHeight or 2) + previewRoot.Size.Y / 2, position.Z)
		model:PivotTo(environment.CFrame.lookAt(center, environment.Vector3.new(frame.Position.X, center.Y, frame.Position.Z)))
		model.Parent = GameEffects.Kill.folder(state)
		if title and context.window then
			pcall(function() context.window:Notify({Title = context.translation.tr(title), Text = context.translation.tr(name), Kind = "info", Duration = 2}) end)
		end

		environment.task.wait(0.45)
		if generation and (state.showGen ~= generation or not state.showing) then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end); return end

		character = context.player.Character
		local killer = character and (character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		GameEffects.Kill.play(state, name, options, model, killer)
		environment.task.wait(2.6 / state.speed)
		pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.clearShow(state)
	for _, folder in {state.folder, state.bodies} do
		if not folder then continue end
		for _, child in folder:GetChildren() do pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
	end
	pcall(GameEffects.Kill.resetShake, state)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.showLabel(state, showing)
	local button, environment = state.showButton, state.context.environment
	if environment.typeof(button) ~= "Instance" then return end

	local start = state.context.translation.tr("Show All Effects")
	local stop = state.context.translation.tr("Stop Showing")
	local text = showing and stop or start
	local objects = button:GetDescendants()
	table.insert(objects, button)
	for _, object in objects do
		if not object:IsA("TextLabel") and not object:IsA("TextButton") then continue end
		if object.Text == start or object.Text == stop then object.Text = text end
	end
end

function GameEffects.Kill.previewAll(state)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	state.showGen += 1
	local generation = state.showGen
	if state.showing then
		state.showing = false
		GameEffects.Kill.clearShow(state)
		environment.task.spawn(function()
			for _ = 1, 16 do
				environment.task.wait(0.2)
				if state.showing or state.showGen ~= generation then break end
				GameEffects.Kill.clearShow(state)
			end
		end)
		GameEffects.Kill.showLabel(state, false)
		return
	end

	state.showing = true
	GameEffects.Kill.showLabel(state, true)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		local function active() return state.alive and state.showing and state.showGen == generation end
		for _, group in {{GameEffects.Kill.knifeEffects, "Knife Kill"}, {GameEffects.Kill.gunEffects, "Gun Kill"}} do
			for _, name in group[1] do
				if not active() then break end
				GameEffects.Kill.preview(state, name, group[1], group[2], generation)
				local started = environment.os.clock()
				while active() and environment.os.clock() - started < 3.2 / state.speed do environment.task.wait(0.05) end
			end
		end
		if state.showGen == generation then
			state.showing = false
			GameEffects.Kill.showLabel(state, false)
		end
	end)
end

function GameEffects.Kill.options(names)
	local options = {}
	for _, name in names do
		table.insert(options, {code = name, label = name:gsub("(%l)(%u)", "%1 %2")})
	end
	table.insert(options, {code = "Random", label = "Random"})
	return options
end

function GameEffects.Kill.install(context)
	local environment, section = context.environment, context.section
	local globals = environment.getgenv()
	if type(globals.__pulseKillFXStop) == "function" then pcall(globals.__pulseKillFXStop) end

	local state = GameEffects.Kill.create(context)
	state.showGen = 0
	state.stop = function() GameEffects.Kill.stop(state) end
	globals.__pulseKillFX, globals.__pulseKillFXStop = state, state.stop
	GameEffects.Kill.observe(state)

	section:Group({Title = "Kill Effects", Side = "Right"})
	section._curSub = "KillFX"
	section:Toggle({Text = "Kill Effects", Default = false, Callback = function(value) state.on = value == true end})
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Knife Kill", Default = "Slash", Options = GameEffects.Kill.options(GameEffects.Kill.knifeEffects), Callback = function(value)
		if value then state.knife = value end
	end})
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Gun Kill", Default = "Headshot", Options = GameEffects.Kill.options(GameEffects.Kill.gunEffects), Callback = function(value)
		if value then state.gun = value end
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Screen Shake", Default = true, Callback = function(value)
		state.shake = value == true
		if not state.shake then GameEffects.Kill.resetShake(state) end
	end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Only My Kills", Default = false, Callback = function(value) state.mine = value == true end})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Kill Sounds", Default = true, Callback = function(value) state.sound = value == true end, Settings = function()
		section:Slider({Text = "Kill Volume", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 60, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value) state.volume = value end})
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Preview Knife Kill", Callback = function() GameEffects.Kill.preview(state, state.knife, GameEffects.Kill.knifeEffects, "Knife Kill") end})
	section:Button({Text = "Preview Gun Kill", Callback = function() GameEffects.Kill.preview(state, state.gun, GameEffects.Kill.gunEffects, "Gun Kill") end})
	state.showButton = section:Button({Text = "Show All Effects", Callback = function() GameEffects.Kill.previewAll(state) end})
	return state.stop
end

local AuraEngine = {}

	FI = "Fire", SM = "Smoke", SP = "Sparkles", F = "Folder", PT = "Part", SH = "SpecialMesh", HL = "Highlight", MD = "Model"}
local AURA_HAT_EFFECTS = {P = true, B = true, T = true, L = true, S = true, U = true}

function AuraEngine.decode(environment, value)
